# Bibliography

Generated from `sources.jsonl`. Reliability r1 (weak) – r5 (authoritative). Status: *read* = opened and read during compilation; *confirmed* = existence and details checked against a catalogue or the source's own page; *unverified* = not checked, verify before relying on it.

## Books

- **[@alter2004]** Alter, J.S. *Yoga in Modern India: The Body between Science and Philosophy*. Princeton University Press. 2004. <https://openlibrary.org/works/OL455675W> (r4, confirmed, scholarly/cultural, cited ×4) — Anthropology of 'scientific yoga' (Kuvalayananda, Kaivalyadhama); context for yoga-nidra lab claims
- **[@weintraub2012]** Amy Weintraub. *Yoga Skills for Therapists: Effective Practices for Mood Management*. W. W. Norton (Norton Professional Books). 2012. <https://wwnorton.co.uk/books/9780393707175-yoga-skills-for-therapists> (r3, confirmed, practitioner, cited ×2) — ISBN 978-0-393-70717-5; x+225 pp. One chapter 'Relax: yoga nidra & iRest'. Not in sources.jsonl at all (grep for weintraub/lifeforce returned nothing).
- **[@weintraub2003]** Amy Weintraub. *Yoga for Depression: A Compassionate Guide to Relieve Suffering Through Yoga*. Broadway Books. 2003. <https://openlibrary.org/works/OL15841220W> (r3, read) — Open Library editions: Broadway Dec 2003 (ISBN 9780767914505), 2004 printings. Nidra content not checked.
- **[@call1891a]** Annie Payson Call. *Power through repose (Roberts Brothers, 1891 printing; Yale Medical Heritage Library scan)*. Roberts Brothers, Boston. 1891. <https://archive.org/details/39002086472298.med.yale.edu> (r5, confirmed) — 1891-dated title page; pagination matches the 1892 printing for pp. 70, 99, 102 and the contents page.
- **[@call1900]** Annie Payson Call. *Power through repose. New Edition with Additions*. Little, Brown and Company, Boston. 1900. <https://archive.org/details/powerthroughrep03callgoog> (r5, confirmed) — Copyright 1900 Little, Brown; adds Ch. XVII–XIX (XIX The Use of the Will, p. 187). Text source for the LibriVox project and probably for Gutenberg #4337.
- **[@bartos2021]** Bartos, S. *Modern Transnational Yoga*. Routledge (DOI 10.4324/9781000367942). 2021. <https://doi.org/10.4324/9781000367942> (r4, read, scholarly/historical) — Found incidentally in Crossref and not used in this summary. Has a chapter 'Satyananda and the Bihar School of Yoga' (pp. 69-98), probably relevant to the project's history and lineages chapter; check it.
- **[@benson1975]** Benson, H. *The Relaxation Response*. William Morrow. 1975. <https://openlibrary.org/works/OL3236792W> (r4, confirmed, scholarly/popular, cited ×2)
- **[@bhattacharya1969]** Bhattacharya, R.S. *Nidrā yā suṣupti*. 1969. <https://openlibrary.org/works/OL348179W> (r3, confirmed, historical/cultural, hi, cited ×2) — Hindi monograph on sleep in yoga (Open Library subjects: Yoga, Sleep); 69 pp.; content not read
- **[@bhattacharya1985]** Bhattacharya, R.S. *An Introduction to the Yogasūtra*. Bharatiya Vidya Prakashan, Delhi. 1985. <https://openlibrary.org/works/OL348177W> (r3, read, scholarly) — Seen only in the Open Library author listing and as the source cited in the LC name authority (t.p.: 'Ram Shankar Bhattacharya, M.A., Vyakaranacharya'). Possibly the author's English treatment of YS 1.10 (sleep as vṛtti), so an English route to his views on nidrā. Not opened; verify content.
- **[@bonnasse2015fr]** Bonnasse, P. *Yoga-nidrâ: la pratique du sommeil conscient*. Almora (coll. Spirit Pratique). 2015. <https://www.almora.fr/product/show/9782351182383/yoganidra-la-pratique-du-sommeil-conscient> (r3, read, practitioner/cultural, fr, cited ×10) — French original of bonnasse2017. Publisher page read: says yoga nidra 'a inspiré la sophrologie' (claim, not checked)
- **[@bonnasse2016]** Bonnasse, P. *Yoga Nidra: Beyond Wakefulness, Dreams and Deep Sleep*. Zen Publications, Mumbai. 2016. <https://www.nidra-yoga.com/yoga-nidra/> (r3, read, practitioner) — Earlier Indian English edition of the 2015 French book, listed on the author's site. ISBN 9789385902215 (Bagchee listing). Not verified whether it uses the same translation as Inner Traditions 2017. Register as an edition of bonnasse2015fr, not a separate work.
- **[@bonnasse2019]** Bonnasse, P. *Yoga-Nidrā, 108 pratiques à conjuguer pour s'éveiller à l'infini*. Les Deux Océans, Paris (coll. Yoga intérieur). 2019. <https://www.nidra-yoga.com/biblio-livres/yoga-nidra-108-pratiques/> (r3, read, practitioner, fr, cited ×1) — The author's much larger later yoga nidra manual (542 pp per a press quote on the author's site): 108 guided micro-practices to combine into sessions. Manual for his 120-hour Yoga-Nidra teacher training at Rishi Yoga Shala. Not translated into English, as far as found.
- **[@bonnasse2023]** Bonnasse, P. *Yoganidrā, la légende de la Déesse*. Almora, Paris. 2023. <https://www.nidra-yoga.com/publications/livres/> (r3, read, cultural, fr) — Listed on the author's site only; not opened. Apparently about the goddess Yoganidrā (compare Devī Māhātmya). Possibly relevant to the goddess section of the history chapter.
- **[@bonnasse2023a]** Bonnasse, P. *Yoganidrā, la légende de la Déesse: le pèlerinage vers la conscience et la joie d'être*. Almora (coll. Les Deux Océans), Paris. 2023. <https://www.editions-tredaniel.com/yoganidr257-p-11220.html> (r3, confirmed, practitioner/cultural, fr) — Oct 2023, 608 pp., ISBN 978-2-35118-624-4, €19.90. Free adaptations of Purāṇic and Śākta legends of the goddess Yoganidrā, with practices. Only the publisher's and author's pages were seen. The French counterpart to Dinsmore-Tuli's goddess material.
- **[@bonnasse2017]** Bonnasse, Pierre. *Yoga Nidra Meditation: The Sleep of the Sages*. Inner Traditions. 2017. <https://www.simonandschuster.com/books/Yoga-Nidra-Meditation/Pierre-Bonnasse/9781620556771> (r3, confirmed, practitioner/cultural, cited ×4) — Translated from French (Karina Bharucha); four complete sessions + how to build your own; Hindu/Buddhist/Tantric framing. Confirmed on Open Library OL19722408W (publisher page 403)
- **[@boyd1976]** Boyd, D. *Swami*. 1976. (r3, *unverified*, historical/practitioner) — Not opened. Cited in Beyond Biofeedback (p. 159) as Boyd's recollections of Swami Rama in Topeka (1970) and of other yogis in India. Boyd was the Greens' son and Swami Rama's research assistant. The other near-first-hand narrative, and a possible source of the popular delta story. Check publisher and the relevant pages.
- **[@boyes1976]** Boyes, D. *Autogenes Yoga: Tiefenentspannung im Liegen, praktische Übungsanleitungen für jedermann*. O.W. Barth, München (tr. Liselotte Klahre); later Heyne 1978, Barth 1983/1985, Econ-Taschenbuch 1986/1989. 1976. <https://d-nb.info/760202095> (r3, confirmed, historical, de, cited ×1) — German translation of boyes1973, confirmed via DNB SRU catalogue data. 158 pp. (1976), 125 pp. (Heyne 1978), 173 pp. (1983 onward). The 1983/1985 Barth records describe it as a combination of time-tested yoga exercises with autogenic training. Not opened as a book.
- **[@boyes1973a]** Boyes, D. *Yoga et ésotérisme: yoga et astrologie, anomalies de l'enfance, souffles vitaux, exotérisme et ésotérisme*. Épi, Paris. 1973. <https://catalogue.bnf.fr/ark:/12148/cb35379581d> (r2, confirmed, historical, fr) — Confirmed via the BnF author list; not read. Context for the esoteric part 4 of boyes1973 (triangles, Masters).
- **[@boyes1973]** Boyes, Dennis. *Le yoga du sommeil éveillé: méthode de relaxation, yoga nidra*. Épi. 1973. <https://gallica.bnf.fr/ark:/12148/bpt6k3362716v.texteImage> (r3, confirmed, historical/cultural, fr, cited ×16) — French; first known modern book presenting yoga nidra as systematic relaxation, before Satyananda 1976 (per Wikipedia); revised ed. 1983; on Gallica
- **[@brenneisen2021]** Brenneisen, N. *Yoga Nidra in der Schwangerschaft*. Windpferd, Aitrang. 2021. <https://d-nb.info/1231934867> (r2, confirmed, practitioner, de) — ISBN 978-3-86410-322-3, 127 pp., foreword by Barbara Kündig. Seen only in DNB SRU results and as a related title on Windpferd pages; the record page itself was not opened.
- **[@brody2019]** Brody, K. *Durf te rusten: win je kracht terug met yoga nidra rustmeditatie*. Samsara, Amsterdam. 2019. <http://resolver.kb.nl/resolve?urn=PPN:419837574> (r3, confirmed, practitioner, nl) — Dutch translation of Daring to Rest by Vera Groen; 255 pp., 23 cm, ISBN 978-94-92995-16-2 (paperback); with bibliography (KB catalogue record). Whether it has Dutch audio is not stated.
- **[@brody2017]** Brody, Karen. *Daring to Rest: Reclaim Your Power with Yoga Nidra Rest Meditation*. Sounds True. 2017. <https://openlibrary.org/works/OL19725633W> (r3, confirmed, practitioner/popular, cited ×3)
- **[@brooks1922]** Brooks, C. H. *The Practice of Autosuggestion by the Method of Emile Coué (revised edition, foreword by Coué)*. Dodd, Mead and Company (New York); Project Gutenberg eBook #29339. 1922. <https://www.gutenberg.org/ebooks/29339> (r3, confirmed, historical) — Public domain. The popular English source for Baudouin's 'outcropping of the subconscious': the unconscious is most accessible just before sleep and just after waking, so the formula is said then. Also gives the 'law of reversed effort', the knotted-string ritual (repeat aloud, let the mind wander) and rules for particular suggestions: don't overreach ('I can hear perfectly'), avoid naming the ailment, use positive form. Cite this, not Coué, for the drowsy-state theory behind the sankalpa.
- **[@call1891]** Call, A. P. *Power Through Repose*. Public domain; Project Gutenberg eBook #4337. 1891. <https://www.gutenberg.org/ebooks/4337> (r4, confirmed, historical, cited ×4) — Primary source for the Western 'proprioceptive relaxation' current (cf. Singleton 2005). The full text contains neither 'nidra' nor 'yoga', which contradicts Shafer's claim (p. 107) that Call described 'Nidra' as pure awareness.
- **[@caycedo1979]** Caycedo, A. *L'aventure de la sophrologie*. Retz, Paris (interviews recorded and translated by Yves Davrou; reissued Le Moustier 1986). 1979. <https://catalogue.bnf.fr/ark:/12148/cb346242288> (r3, confirmed, historical, fr) — 205 pp. The founder's own account of sophrology's origins, phenomenology, and the Plato Charmides source of 'terpnos logos' (p. 94, per Wikipedia). Existence confirmed in the BnF; not read.
- **[@caycedo1966]** Caycedo, A. *India of yogi*. published in India (details not verified). 1966. <https://books.google.fr/books?id=ba9WAAAAMAAJ> (r2, *unverified*, historical) — Caycedo's own account of his time with yogis, the direct primary source for what he took from yoga. Known only from a French Wikipedia citation; not opened. A companion volume is 'Letters of silence'.
- **[@church2018]** Church, D. *Mind to Matter: The Astonishing Science of How Your Brain Creates Material Reality*. Hay House. 2018. (r1, *unverified*, popular) — Named by Lusk as the source of most of her brainwave/DNA/telomere claims (2019 web article). Not a yoga nidra book; useful only as an example of the pop-science source chain to avoid. Not seen.
- **[@caycedo1998]** Chéné, P.-A. (preface and code of ethics by A. Caycedo). *Sophrologie, tome 1: fondements et méthodologie*. Ellébore, Paris (3rd ed., Thesus collection), 423 pp., ISBN 2-86898-505-X. 1998. <https://catalogue.bnf.fr/ark:/12148/cb37076573t> (r3, confirmed, practitioner/cultural, fr, cited ×4) — BnF record opened 2026-10-04: 3rd revised and enlarged ed., 1998. Sophrology founded by Caycedo in Madrid 1960; his Asian travels are dated variously (mid-1960s; e.g. 1965-67 on French Wikipedia, 1964-68 in other sources).
- **[@coue1922]** Coué, É. *Self Mastery Through Conscious Autosuggestion*. 1922. <https://openlibrary.org/works/OL8792716W> (r3, confirmed, historical/popular, cited ×5) — Autosuggestion formula; parallel to sankalpa
- **[@demichelis2004]** De Michelis, E. *A History of Modern Yoga: Patañjali and Western Esotericism*. Continuum (Bloomsbury). 2004. <https://doi.org/10.5040/9781472548436> (r5, confirmed, scholarly/historical, cited ×4) — Classic typology of modern yoga; relaxation and 'healing ritual of secular religion'; confirmed via Crossref chapter DOIs
- **[@desai2025]** Desai, K. *Yoga Nidra: The Art of Transformational Sleep (Restore your Health, Reshape your Life and Change your Destiny), Indian edition*. Motilal Banarsidass Publishing House. 2025. <https://www.bagchee.com/books/BB139380/yoga-nidra-the-art-of-transformational-sleep-restore-your-health-reshape-your-life-and-change-your-destiny> (r3, confirmed, practitioner) — The 'newer printing': Bagchee lists 23 Aug 2025, 1st ed., 406 pp., ISBN 9789368531180; Google Books (id 5hhzEQAAQBAJ) gives ISBN 9789368539773 and a contents list with page numbers (Section Three = pp. 341-363). Probably a reprint of the 2017 text, not checked.
- **[@desai]** Desai, K. *Yoga Nidra - French Edition (ebook)*. Lotus Press. <https://www.lotuspress.com/products/yoga-nidra-french-edition-ebook-984343> (r3, read, practitioner, fr) — French translation sold as an ebook, ISBN 9781608692897. French title, translator and year not shown on the page.
- **[@desai2017]** Desai, Kamini. *Yoga Nidra: The Art of Transformational Sleep*. Lotus Press. 2017. <https://openlibrary.org/works/OL20053517W> (r3, confirmed, practitioner, cited ×5)
- **[@descamps2015]** Descamps, M.-A. *Yoga-nidra et rêve éveillé*. Éditions Accarias-L'Originel, Paris (reprinted 2023, ISBN 978-2-86316-365-8). 2015. <https://loriginel.com/produit/yoga-nidra-et-reve-eveille-de-marc-alain-descamps/> (r3, confirmed, practitioner, fr, cited ×1) — Suggested key descamps2015. ISBN 978-2-86316-258-3 (26 June 2015), 157 pp., ill., glossary, 2-p. bibliography; BnF ark cb44374267s; SUDOC PPN 188071717 covers both ISBNs (reprint, same edition). No preview, no ebook, no audio. Publisher confirmed (now trades as L'Originel, loriginel.com).
- **[@descamps1999]** Descamps, M.-A. *Le rêve éveillé*. Bernet-Danilo, Meschers (coll. Essentialis 48). 1999. <https://catalogue.bnf.fr/ark:/12148/cb37178472v> (r3, confirmed, practitioner, fr) — 61-page booklet; the author's own short account of the waking dream, likely the basis of the waking-dream half of descamps2015. Catalogue record only.
- **[@descamps2006]** Descamps, M.-A. *Les rêves : les comprendre et les diriger*. Dangles, Escalquens (coll. Psycho-nova). 2006. <https://catalogue.bnf.fr/ark:/12148/cb40182134c> (r3, confirmed, practitioner, fr) — 237 pp. Shows the author's dream-direction background. Catalogue record only.
- **[@descamps1983]** Descamps, M.-A. *La maîtrise des rêves*. Éditions universitaires, Paris (Encyclopédie universitaire). 1983. <https://catalogue.bnf.fr/ark:/12148/cb34725814n> (r3, confirmed, scholarly, fr) — 220 pp. Earlier academic book on mastering/directing dreams. Catalogue record only.
- **[@descamps2011]** Descamps, M.-A. *Histoire du hatha-yoga : en France, passé et présent*. Almora, Paris. 2011. <https://catalogue.bnf.fr/ark:/12148/cb425470662> (r3, confirmed, historical, fr) — 158 pp., ISBN 978-2-35118-070-9. Possible source for French yoga nidra history (Boyes, sophrology); contents not checked.
- **[@descamps2014]** Descamps, M.-A. *La méditation : apprendre à méditer vraiment*. Éditions Accarias-L'Originel, Paris. 2014. <https://loriginel.com/produit/la-meditation-de-marc-alain-descamps/> (r2, confirmed, practitioner, fr) — 155 pp., ISBN 978-2-86316-245-3 (current SKU 9782863162897). Sibling volume of descamps2015; low relevance.
- **[@desoille1945]** Desoille, R. *Le rêve éveillé en psychothérapie : essai sur la fonction de régulation de l'inconscient collectif*. Presses universitaires de France, Paris. 1945. <https://catalogue.bnf.fr/ark:/12148/cb320232261> (r4, confirmed, historical, fr) — Suggested key desoille1945. 388 pp. Primary source for the waking dream and its Jungian (collective unconscious) framing; cite for any yoga nidra / rêve éveillé comparison.
- **[@desoille1961]** Desoille, R. *Théorie et pratique du rêve éveillé dirigé*. Éditions du Mont-Blanc, Genève. 1961. <https://catalogue.bnf.fr/ark:/12148/cb35220005j> (r4, confirmed, historical, fr) — Suggested key desoille1961. 216 pp. Desoille's mature statement of the directed waking dream method.
- **[@dikshit1967]** Dikshit, K.N. *Yoga-nidrā*. 1967. <https://openlibrary.org/works/OL328871W> (r2, confirmed, historical/cultural, hi, cited ×3) — Hindi poetry on Krishna (Open Library subjects): the mythological sense of the word, nine years before Satyananda's book; content not read
- **[@dinsmoretuli2014]** Dinsmore-Tuli, Uma. *Yoni Shakti: A Woman's Guide to Power and Freedom through Yoga and Tantra*. Yogawords. 2014. <https://openlibrary.org/works/OL19982292W> (r3, confirmed, practitioner, cited ×1) — Women's health yoga; source of the menstrual/life-cycle nidras. Confirmed on Open Library (2014)
- **[@dinsmoretuli2020]** Dinsmore-Tuli, Uma & Tuli, Nirlipta. *Yoga Nidra Made Easy*. Hay House UK. 2022. <https://openlibrary.org/works/OL25474263W> (r3, confirmed, practitioner/popular, cited ×6) — First published 2022 (Open Library: Hay House UK, 2022, c. 257 pp.); the id's 2020 is historical. 44 free audio recordings on the Network's site.
- **[@dinsmoretuli2022]** Dinsmore-Tuli, Uma (with contributions by Nirlipta Tuli). *Nidrā Shakti: The Power of Rest – An Illustrated Encyclopaedia of Yoga Nidrā*. Sitaram & Sons Ltd (Yoga Nidra Network). 2024. <https://www.yoganidranetwork.org/product/nidra-shakti/> (r4, confirmed, practitioner/critical, cited ×19) — Publisher page (opened 2026-10-04): 2024, Sitaram & Sons Ltd, hardback, 'compiled and written by' Uma Dinsmore-Tuli with contributions by Nirlipta Tuli; A-Z, histories, research summaries, 50+ contributors, 108 transcribed practices. Over 700 pages (Arewa 2025 review; shop data 704 pp.). The id's 2022 is the year of the excerpt and mockup. Content barely read (see reading/dinsmoretuli2022.md).
- **[@dumas2024]** Dumas, J. & Sansregret, L. *Mon yoga nidra: apprendre à relâcher ses tensions physiques, émotionnelles et mentales*. Flammarion, Paris (coll. Santé, bien-être). 2024. <https://editions.flammarion.com/mon-yoga-nidra/9782080459510> (r2, confirmed, practitioner/popular, fr, cited ×1) — Published 16 Oct 2024. ISBN 978-2-08-045951-0 (paperback, 224 pp.; BnF counts 221); e-book 978-2-08-045952-7. 15 themed sessions (Apaiser / Activer / Modifier), 13 printed as 8-stage scripts, each with a mudra, plus 2 audio only. Closing chapter on writing your own sessions. Prints a 12-stage 'long version' (p. 21). Unsupported claims: 3-4 h of sleep per session (p. 13); yoga nidra as the 'origin' of meditation, self-hypnosis, EMDR and sophrology (p. 15). No bibliography. Project id dumas2024.
- **[@dumas2026]** Dumas, J. & Sansregret, L. *Nidra: Yoga antistress*. J'ai lu, Paris (Bien-être no. 14626). 2026. <https://www.jailu.com/nidra/9782290425466> (r2, confirmed, popular, fr) — Retitled pocket reissue of Mon yoga nidra (the copyright page says so). Published 11 Mar 2026, ISBN 978-2-290-42546-6, 256 pp. (BnF counts 252), 8.30 EUR. Section titles changed to 'Les séances pour changer' and 'Créer mes Nidra-histoires'; same text with light copy-editing. Do not count separately from dumas2024.
- **[@dumas2019]** Dumas, J. & Sansregret, L. *Mudra: le yoga des doigts*. Flammarion (rev. ed. 2023; J'ai lu 2022). 2019. <https://catalogue.bnf.fr/ark:/12148/cb458015872> (r2, read, practitioner/popular, fr) — The authors' mudra book, which Mon yoga nidra refers readers to for the mudras paired with each session. Seen in catalogue records only.
- **[@dumas2025]** Dumas, J. & Sansregret, L. *Yogathérapie: le yoga au service de votre santé*. Flammarion. 2025. <https://editions.flammarion.com/Auteurs/sansregret-locana> (r2, read, practitioner/popular, fr) — 311 pp. (BnF). The publisher sample (https://www.edenlivres.fr/p/815856) lists 'relaxation et Nidra' among yoga therapy's tools. Its framework is Raja yoga and the koshas. Checked only for yoga nidra content.
- **[@esposito2018]** Esposito, R. *La sophrologie (Que sais-je ? n° 4080)*. Presses universitaires de France. 2018. <https://shs.cairn.info/la-sophrologie--9782715422933?lang=fr> (r3, *unverified*, scholarly, fr) — A short overview, used by Wikipedia and Inserm for Caycedo's journey (1965-67), his yoga-practising wife, and the 'several sophrologies'. Not opened.
- **[@fabre1985]** Fabre, N. & Maurey, G. *Le rêve éveillé analytique*. Privat, Toulouse. 1985. <https://fr.wikipedia.org/wiki/R%C3%AAve_%C3%A9veill%C3%A9_dirig%C3%A9> (r3, *unverified*, practitioner, fr) — Seen only as a citation in French Wikipedia (pp. 125-139, source of the '3 V' criteria: voir, vivre, verbaliser). Not catalogue-checked.
- **[@farias2015]** Farias, M. & Wikholm, C. *The Buddha Pill: Can Meditation Change You?* Watkins. 2015. <https://openlibrary.org/works/OL28767602W> (r4, confirmed, critical/popular, cited ×2)
- **[@green1977]** Green, E. & Green, A. *Beyond Biofeedback*. Delacorte Press, New York. 1977. <https://openlibrary.org/search?q=beyond+biofeedback+green> (r3, confirmed, scholarly, cited ×4) — Bibliographic data confirmed (Open Library: Delacorte/Knoll 1977, 369 pp.); full text read via the Green Foundation PDF (reading/green1977.md). Does not report the 1970 'yogic sleep' session; cite green1974 for that.
- **[@gurumaa2021]** Gurumaa, A. *Yoga Nidra: Ancient Yogic Technique of Total Transformation*. Gurumaa Vani (GM Vani), Rishi Chaitanya Ashram, Ganaur. 2020. <https://www.gurumaa.com/store/books/yoga-nidra-english-book> (r2, read, practitioner, cited ×1) — ISBN 978-93-81464-64-9. Amazon.in gives 20 Dec 2020, 140 pp.; Exotic India lists a 2023 printing of 152 pp. Pocket paperback. Satyananda-format practice presented as an ancient tantric method; Vedanta and guru framing. Ch. 7 and the appendix report the ICMR-NIN hypertension pilot (Devraj 2021). Goodreads credits Urmila Pandey as translator/compiler. E-copy only in the Amrit Varsha app. Look-inside read: TOC, pp. 13-14, 31-33, 56-57, 80-81.
- **[@gurumaa2022]** Gurumaa, A. *योग निद्रा: सम्पूर्ण रूपान्तरण की पुरातन पद्धति (Yog Nidra: Sampurn Rupantaran ki Puratan Paddhati)*. Gurumaa Vani (गुरुमाँ वाणी). 2022. <https://www.gurumaa.com/store/books/yoga-nidra-hindi-book> (r2, read, practitioner, hi) — Hindi edition of gurumaa2021. ISBN 978-93-81464-76-2. Amazon.in date 1 Jan 2022, 140 pp.; Exotic India lists 153 pp. Same chapters and appendix. Look-inside pp. 24-25 give the sankalpa stage (examples, three times, start and end, 'certainly fulfilled') and the rotation stage (right thumb first; mental Om).
- **[@gottinger2025]** Göttinger, L. *Yoga Nidra – die Kunst der tiefen Entspannung: Wie du dein Nervensystem beruhigst, um besser zu schlafen, klarer zu denken und gelassener durchs Leben zu gehen. Mit Audioanleitungen*. riva Verlag (Münchner Verlagsgruppe), München. 2025. <https://www.m-vg.de/riva/shop/article/25220-yoga-nidra-die-kunst-der-tiefen-entspannung/> (r3, confirmed, practitioner/popular, de) — This is the book summarised; the project registry has no entry yet (registry_id null). Print ISBN 978-3-7423-2802-1; e-book 978-3-7453-2610-9. 176 pp., Feb 2025, 2. Auflage 2026. Eight guided practices with QR-code audio narrated by the author. Publisher reading sample: https://www.book2look.de/embed/9783742328021. Scanned TOC (hbz): https://digitale-objekte.hbz-nrw.de/storage2/2025/04/17/file_9/9866097.pdf. Author is Dr. med. Lan Göttinger, Fachärztin für Allgemeinmedizin (GND 1357707193).
- **[@goettinger2025]** Göttinger, L. *Yoga Nidra – die Kunst der tiefen Entspannung*. 2025. (r3, confirmed, practitioner, de, cited ×2)
- **[@nagendra2000]** H. R. Nagendra & R. Nagarathna. *New Perspectives in Stress Management. Revised (2000), Internet Archive scan*. Swami Vivekananda Yoga Prakashana. 2000. <https://archive.org/details/sochara.14907.newperspectivesi0000nage> (r4, read) — Access-restricted (printdisabled lending); 136 pp. per Open Library OL59042037M; only the metadata was checked.
- **[@hersey2024]** Hersey, T. *We Will Rest! The Art of Escape*. Hachette Book Group (imprint not verified). 2024. <https://thenapministry.wordpress.com/2024/11/09/we-will-rest-the-art-of-escape/> (r3, read, cultural) — Sequel, published 12 Nov 2024 per the author's blog. Described as 'meditations and poetry' with storytelling and art, modelled on hymnals and abolitionist pamphlets. May hold more guided material than the 2022 book. Not examined.
- **[@hersey2023]** Hersey, T. *The Nap Ministry's Rest Deck: 50 Practices to Resist Grind Culture*. unverified. 2023. <https://www.usatoday.com/booklist/books/the-nap-ministrys-rest-deck-50-practices-to-resist-grind-culture/9781797215761> (r2, *unverified*, practitioner) — Card deck of 50 rest practices (ISBN 9781797215761), known only from Wikipedia citing USA Today. Not opened.
- **[@hersey2022]** Hersey, Tricia. *Rest Is Resistance: A Manifesto*. Little, Brown Spark. 2022. <https://www.hachettebookgroup.com/titles/tricia-hersey/rest-is-resistance/9780316365536/> (r3, confirmed, critical/popular/cultural, cited ×4) — Not a yoga nidra book; the key text of the 'rest as resistance' movement (The Nap Ministry) that shapes how Stanley, Brody and others frame rest. Publisher page title confirmed (Hachette)
- **[@compiler]** Himalayan Yoga Publications Trust (compiler). *Yogi in the Lab: Future Directions of Scientific Research in Meditation*. Himalayan Yoga Publications Trust. <https://www.yogapublications.org/product-page/yogi-in-the-lab> (r3, read, practitioner/historical) — Only the description was seen: articles on laboratory research on Swami Rama and Swami Veda, including 'the use of yoga nidra in place of anaesthesia'. Rs 150. Subtitle taken from an Exotic India search-result title; not checked. Year and editor not seen. Probably the best source for the lineage's lab claims.
- **[@jacobson1929]** Jacobson, E. *Progressive Relaxation*. University of Chicago Press. 1929. <https://openlibrary.org/works/OL1590356W> (r4, confirmed, historical/scholarly, cited ×3)
- **[@jacobson1934]** Jacobson, E. *You Must Relax: A Practical Method of Reducing the Strains of Modern Living*. McGraw-Hill (Whittlesey House). 1934. <https://archive.org/details/bwb_W9-CIN-469> (r4, read, historical) — Popular companion to Progressive Relaxation; the edition Dinsmore-Tuli and Singleton cite. Internet Archive controlled lending only (several printings). German translation: 'Entspannung als Therapie' (Klett-Cotta, 7th ed. 2011, 218 pp., ISBN 978-3-608-89112-6; DNB confirms original title). Publisher imprint per Nathoo 2016 (McGraw-Hill); Whittlesey House is McGraw-Hill's trade imprint, unverified for this title.
- **[@jain2014]** Jain, A.R. *Selling Yoga: From Counterculture to Pop Culture*. Oxford University Press. 2014. <https://openlibrary.org/works/OL20011754W> (r4, confirmed, scholarly/critical/industry, cited ×2) — Yoga as consumer product; frame for NSDR/app commercialisation

General Scandinavian-school yoga manual. Yoga nidra is a 1-2 page section in ch. 9 (pratyahara), p. 99 in the German 2016 layout. Not read; no free text.

Whether it contains yoga nidra is unverified.

Sheila La Farge from the Swedish, 112 pp., ISBN 0345254465, LCCN 76013536. Also Rider, London 1978 (112 pp.). Contents not seen.

Borrowable via Internet Archive controlled lending (not read). Google Books snippet view only.
- **[@janakanandasaraswati2021]** Janakananda Saraswati, S. *En djupare yoga och Yoga Nidra (English: A Deeper Yoga and Yoga Nidra)*. Förlaget Bindu, Hamneda (EPUB3 with embedded audio; ISBN 9789198590210 sv; English ebook ISBN 9789198590234; Danish paperback 2024, 130 pp, ISBN 9789198881455; Swedish paperback 2026, 134 pp, ISBN 9789198936506). 2021. <https://samplefiles.elib.se/epub/1134535/1134535_202104221705.epub> (r4, read, practitioner, sv) — Publisher's sample file (Provfil från Bindu Förlag) read: copyright line 1983, 2013, 2021; full table of contents (sections on sankalpa, nyasa, sleep and wakefulness, staying awake, coming out again, mountain symbol, four animals, shape of the egg, teaching yoga nidra, Satyananda); track timings 20:05 / 12:28 / 43:42; advises starting with the short practice. The expanded successor of the CD booklet; this is project item janakananda2021.
- **[@janakananda2021]** Janakananda Saraswati, Swami. *A Deeper Yoga and Yoga Nidra: and the Soundtracks of the Guided Deep Relaxations from Experience Yoga Nidra*. Bindu Publishers / Förlaget Bindu, Hamneda. 2021. <https://yogameditationshop.com/collections/downloads/products/a-deeper-yoga-and-yoga-nidra> (r4, read, practitioner, cited ×1) — EPUB 3 e-book with embedded audio, ISBN 978-91-985902-3-4, Apple release 22 Apr 2021 (~84 Apple pages, 105 MB). Swedish e-book ISBN 978-91-985902-1-0 (1 Apr 2021); Danish and German e-books 2021; Danish print 2024 (ISBN 978-91-988814-5-5, 130 pp); Swedish print 2026 (ISBN 978-91-989365-0-6, 134 pp, translated from Danish). English paperback with QR codes sold in the school's shop. Audio: 9:00 intro, 20:29 little nidra, 12:28 music, 45:06 deep nidra. Its TOC (seen in the publisher sample) has no printed transcripts. Distinct from janakananda1997 (CD + booklet) and from Let Go (2024).
- **[@janakanandasaraswati2024]** Janakananda Saraswati, Swami. *Let Go: Deep tantric yoga and meditation – and meditating lying down*. Bindu Publishers. 2024. <https://yogameditationshop.com/products/let-go> (r4, read, practitioner) — 380 pp., 4+ hours of audio via QR codes; partly autobiographical (training in India in the late 1960s). Danish edition 'Giv slip' 2023. The publisher offers a free excerpt PDF. Opened only to distinguish it from janakananda2021 (it was rejected as a separate selection).
- **[@ed1992]** Julie T. Lusk (ed.). *30 Scripts for Relaxation, Imagery & Inner Healing (vols. 1-2)*. Lusk's earlier edited script collection; vol. 2 year unverified.
- **[@jyotirananda2017]** Jyotirananda, I.; Vidyanandi, M. *Yoga Nidra y Mindfulness. Ediciones Obelisco, Rubí (Barcelona), series Libros singulares. 2017. <https://www.edicionesobelisco.com/libros-de-salud-y-vida-natural/2583-yoga-nidra-y-mindfulness.html> (r2, read, practitioner/popular, es, cited ×2) — ISBN 978-84-9111-265-5; 1st ed. Nov 2017 (colophon; catalogues also give 31 Oct 2017 and 28 Feb 2018); 104 pp. hardcover with CD (2/15/35-min practices). Civil names on the copyright page: Ignasi Martorell and Montserrat Vidal. Satyananda-trained, independent 'Sistema Darshan' blending yoga nidra, secular mindfulness, NLP and (self-declared) Ericksonian indirect language. The free publisher sample PDF (ToC + chs 1-5) was read: https://www.edicionesobelisco.com/download/pdf_samples/Yoga_Nidra_y_Mindfulness.pdf . Project selection id jyotirananda2018.
- **[@kabatzinn2018]** Kabat-Zinn, J. *Falling Awake: How to Practice Mindfulness in Everyday Life*. 2018. (r3, *unverified*, practitioner) — Seen only as a bibliography entry in Shafer's book (p. 218). Shafer borrows the 'fall awake' idea from it (p. 107). It shares the title, so take care to avoid confusion in citations.
- **[@klein]** Klein, Jean. *Sois ce que tu es*. (r2, *unverified*, fr) — Not opened. Cited by Boyes 1973, p. 28 (Gallica search snippet), on watching persistent images like a film. Documents a reading link between Boyes and Jean Klein, Richard Miller's teacher. Publication details not checked.
- **[@kumar2013]** Kumar, K. *A Handbook of Yoga-Nidrā*. D.K. Printworld, New Delhi (2nd ed. 2018, paperback). 2013. <https://openlibrary.org/works/OL22346181W> (r2, read, practitioner/scholarly, cited ×1) — xiii+146 pp.; foreword Pranav Pandya; HB ISBN 9788124606858, PB 9788124606865; LCCN 2013410953; OCLC 856989073. Read 17 sample pages + TOC/preface/foreword via retailer look-inside. Satyananda-format practice; theory chs 1-8, author's DSVV doctoral data chs 10-16 (t-tests, many inconsistent with printed means/SDs), 6-page practice ch. 18. Repeats misattributed studies (Cooper & Aygen = TM; Carrington 1980; Bali 1979). Cited as ref 15 in pandiperumal2022.

No preview on Google Books or Open Library.
- **[@kuendig]** Kündig, B. *Yoga Nidra: Die Perle der Tiefenentspannung – In 30 Minuten völlig erfrischt (mit CD)*. <https://www.amazon.de/Yoga-Nidra-Die-Perle-Tiefenentspannung/dp/3893856374> (r2, confirmed, practitioner/popular/cultural, de, cited ×4) — Swiss author; English translation 'Yoga Nidra: Blissful Deep Relaxation' (2021, Open Library OL24912803W). - **[@miller2022]** Miller, Richard C. *Yoga Nidra: The iRest Meditative Practice for Deep Relaxation and Healing*. Sounds True, Boulder (distributed by St. Martin's/Macmillan). 2022. <https://books.google.com/books?vid=ISBN9781683648970> (r4, confirmed, practitioner) — Current printing of miller2005 (same work). Paperback ISBN 978-1-68364-897-0, 168 pp.; ebook 978-1-68364-898-7, 162 pp.; published 1 March 2022. Foreword by Lorin Roche; includes a 'Preface to the Second Edition'; ten steps titled after the Oxherding Pictures with kosha subtitles; iRest Yoga Nidra Worksheet; Examples of Opposites; Readings in Nondual Saivism. No CD; audio by free download. Contents and CIP pages seen in the Google Books preview (ebook id 9FOzEAAAQBAJ).

- **[@mishra1961]** Mishra, R. S. *Fundamentals of Yoga (Lancer Books paperback reprint), Google Books snippet view*. Lancer Books, New York (by arrangement with Julian Press); scan via Google Books (Univ. of Virginia copy). n.d. [1961-1973]; copyright 1959. <https://books.google.com/books?id=in0wAAAAYAAJ> (r4, confirmed, historical) — Google labels it Julian Press 1959, 255 pp., but the copyright page shows a Lancer reprint. Search-inside snippets give the full contents (pp. 11-12): yoganidra chapters 3 (p.44), 9 (p.77), 10 (p.83), 15 'Anesthesia Produced by Yoganidra' (pp.106-108). Uses Charcot's states (lethargic/cataleptic/somnambulistic), mesmeric 'magnetism' and posthypnotic-style suggestion.
- **[@mishra1959]** Mishra, Rammurti. *Fundamentals of Yoga*. Julian Press, New York (later paperbacks incl. Lancer). 1959. <https://openlibrary.org/works/OL5268427W> (r3, confirmed, historical/practitioner, cited ×2) — Contains 'Anesthesia produced by yoganidra' (pp. 106-108), cited in Lamb 2006: an early English use of the term before Satyananda
- **[@moore2019]** Moore, Scott. *Practical Yoga Nidra: A 10-Step Method to Reduce Stress, Improve Sleep, and Restore Your Spirit*. Rockridge Press. 2019. <https://www.scottmooreyoga.com/blog/practical-yoga-nidra> (r3, confirmed, practitioner/popular, cited ×2) — Secular 10-step method; year/publisher from memory (ISBN 9781646110285)
- **[@mumford]** Mumford, J. *Psychosomatic Yoga*. (r3, *unverified*, practitioner/historical) — Named by Röcker on her website as one of her yoga nidra sources (German: 'Psychosomatischer Yoga'). Not opened; check edition, year and publisher before citing.
- **[@nagendra1986]** Nagendra, H. R. & Nagarathna, R. *New Perspectives in Stress Management*. Vivekananda Kendra Yoga Anusandhana Samsthan, Bangalore. 1988. (r3, confirmed, practitioner/scholarly, cited ×1) — First edition 1988 per Open Library (112 pp.); the id's 1986 is historical. Revised ed. 2000 (nagendra2000). SVYASA stress-management (SMET) course text naming QRT and DRT; not a yoga nidra book.
- **[@neubauer1972]** Neubauer, R. *Yoga und autogenes Training*. not shown. 1972. <https://books.google.com/books?id=gtbEXwAACAAJ> (r2, read, cultural, de) — Metadata only (184 pp.). Evidence that the German market paired yoga with AT around the time of Boyes's 'Autogenes Yoga' (1976).
- **[@newcombe2019]** Newcombe, S. *Yoga in Britain: Stretching Spirituality and Educating Yogis*. Equinox. 2019. <https://openlibrary.org/works/OL21618637W> (r5, confirmed, scholarly/historical, cited ×3) — How yoga (incl. relaxation) entered adult education in Britain
- **[@olalla2007]** Olalla, N. *Yoga-Nidra: efectos en el cerebro y el sistema nervioso*. Prames, Zaragoza. 2007. <https://anasesmanuez.com/bibliografia-yoga-nidra/> (r2, *unverified*, practitioner, es) — Earlier Spanish-language yoga nidra book, seen only in Sesma's bibliography; check the catalogue record. A possible addition to the Spanish list in 11.
- **[@panda2003]** Panda, N. C. *Yoga-Nidrā: Yogic Trance — Theory, Practice and Applications*. D.K. Printworld, New Delhi (later printings under its Decent Books imprint; 3rd ed. 2018). 2003. <https://test.dkprintworld.com/product/yoga-nidra-%c2%97-yogic-trance/> (r2, confirmed, practitioner, cited ×1) — HB ISBN 978-81-246-0242-3 (81-246-0242-5); PB 978-81-246-0243-0 (81-246-0243-3); xxviii + 324 pp. The author is Nrusingh Charan Panda (LC: Paṇḍā, Nr̥siṃhacaraṇa, 1929-), a retired veterinary nutrition scientist and Odia novelist. No studies or data; disease claims including cancer treatment; prints the full tantric mātṛkā-nyāsa (pp. 271-280); the practice is 12 pages (178-189). Read from 50 retailer sample pages, the full contents and the index.
- **[@panda2003a]** Panda, N. C. *Meditation: Science and Practice*. D.K. Printworld, New Delhi. 2003. <https://test.dkprintworld.com/author-book/n-c-panda/> (r2, read, practitioner) — ISBN 81-246-0211-5 (HB), xvi + 336 pp., per the publisher's back-cover advert on the yoga nidra book. Open Library wrongly files this ISBN as a separate 'Yoga Nidra' work (OL10226319W). Only the advert was seen.
- **[@prakashananda2010]** Prakashananda, S. *Yoga Nidra - Neue Erkenntnisse (2. Aufl.)*. Ananda Verlag. 2010. (r2, *unverified*, practitioner, de) — German Satyananda-lineage yoga nidra book. The dissertation cites it (pp. 30, 39, 70) on the 'yoga nidra state' and on using yoga nidra for night waking. The author names Swami Prakashananda (d. 2020) as her teacher. Seen only as a citation.
- **[@rama1988]** Rama, Swami. *Path of Fire and Light, Vol. 2: A Practical Companion to Volume One*. Himalayan Institute Press, Honesdale PA. 1988. <https://books.google.com/books?id=il5e2hgHarIC> (r3, read, practitioner, cited ×7) — ISBN 0-89389-112-6; (c) 1988, 1996. Edited from Swami Rama's 1987 summer advanced seminar (LoC note). Yoga nidra in ch. 11 'Exercises' pp. 165-182 (61 points pp. 169-171, shithali karana pp. 171-175, yoga nidra pp. 176-182) and ch. 4 pp. 66-76. First-hand Menninger claim p. 166. Google Books mislabels it 'Advanced Practices of Yoga, 1986' (that is Vol. 1). Limited preview: contents and copyright pages, plus search-inside snippets.
- **[@rammbonwitt1984]** Ramm-Bonwitt, I. *Yoga Nidra - der Schlaf der Yogis: ein Weg zum Gleichgewicht von Körper, Seele und Geist*. Bauer, Freiburg im Breisgau (ISBN 3-7626-0615-3). 1984. <https://services.dnb.de/sru/dnb?version=1.1&operation=searchRetrieve&query=per%3DRamm-Bonwitt%20and%20tit%3DNidra&recordSchema=oai_dc&maximumRecords=20> (r3, confirmed, practitioner/historical, de, cited ×2) — DNB catalogue records only; book not read. 143 pp.; cassette 1986. Expanded Schirner edition 'Yoga-Nidra: der Schlaf der Yogis; Körper, Geist und Seele entspannen durch Visualisierung', c. 2006/07, 379 pp., with CD (ISBN 978-3-89767-300-7); new edition 2010 (ISBN 978-3-89767-882-8); separate CD 2006 (ISBN 978-3-89767-340-3). An early German-language yoga nidra book, 23 years before Röcker. Röcker mentions it in the 2021 podcast as appearing 'in parallel' with hers, meaning the Schirner edition.
- **[@rammbonwitt2007]** Ramm-Bonwitt, I. *Yoga-Nidra: Der Schlaf der Yogis. Körper, Geist und Seele entspannen durch Visualisierung (completely revised edition, incl. Audio-CD)*. Schirner, Darmstadt. 2007 (CD 2006); paperback 2010. <https://d-nb.info/1000347567> (r3, confirmed, practitioner/popular/cultural, de) — 379 pp.; ISBN 978-3-89767-300-7 (2007, with CD) and 978-3-89767-882-8 (2010). Not a reprint: fully rewritten, 143 to 379 pp., heavy depth-psychology section. Real ToC of 2010 read (https://d-nb.info/1000347567/04). No longer in Schirner's catalogue (Oct 2026).
- **[@rammbonwitt2011]** Ramm-Bonwitt, I. *Tantrische Yoga-Meditationen: Wege zu innerer Freiheit und geistiger Kraft (publisher's free sample)*. Schirner, Darmstadt. 2011. <https://www.schirner.com/files/leseproben/1010_Leseprobe.pdf> (r3, read, practitioner, de) — Legitimate 24-page sample: ToC (includes 'Antar Mauna') and a full guided meditation (pp. 194-197) showing her guiding style: formal Sie, imperatives plus questions, backward breath count 12 to 0, yoni mudra, trataka.
- **[@roecker]** Röcker, A.E. *Mit Yoga Nidra das Leben meistern*. Via Nova, 2nd ed., ISBN 978-3-86616-069-9. <https://www.verlag-vianova.de/mit-yoga-nidra-das-leben-meistern.html> (r2, read, practitioner/cultural, de, cited ×5) — Publisher page read. Typical German-market framing: alpha-state access to 'the unconscious', explicitly 'not trance'
- **[@satyanandasaraswati1980a]** Satyananda Saraswati, S. *Yoga nidra: apprenez à dormir*. Éditions Satyanandashram, Paris. 1980. <https://catalogue.bnf.fr/ark:/12148/cb34851417d> (r4, confirmed, historical/cultural/practitioner, fr) — 208 pp. French translation of Satyananda's Yoga Nidra, 12 years before the German one (1992). A possible source for Paris-based authors such as Ramm-Bonwitt. 2015 retranslation by Micheline Flak (cb44498458x).
- **[@satyananda1976]** Satyananda Saraswati, Swami. *Yoga Nidra*. Bihar School of Yoga / Yoga Publications Trust. 1976. <https://openlibrary.org/works/OL10424320W> (r4, confirmed, practitioner/historical, cited ×36) — Defining text of the modern practice
- **[@satyanandasaraswati1974a]** Satyananda Saraswati, Swami. *Tantra-yoga panorama*. International Yoga Fellowship Movement. 1974. <https://books.google.com/books?id=UkwvAAAAYAAJ> (r3, *unverified*, practitioner/historical) — Not opened. Cited on Wikipedia (p. 25) for Satyananda's use of yoga nidra with the child Niranjanananda; another 1974 Satyananda text mentioning yoga nidra, so 'earliest in print' claims for Meditations from the Tantras need care.
- **[@satyananda1974]** Satyananda Saraswati, Swami (ed. Nishchalananda Saraswati, Swami). *Meditations from the Tantras, with Live Class Transcriptions*. Front matter, contents and 16 sample pages seen; the yoga nidra chapter was not seen.
- **[@satyananda1976de]** Satyananda Saraswati, Swami (German tr.). *Yoga Nidra (German edition, Ananda Verlag)*. Earlier German ed.: 'Tiefenentspannt durch Yoga Nidra' (ISBN 3928831259). Blurb: 'developed from tantric nyāsa'
- **[@satyanandasaraswati1983]** Satyananda Saraswati, Swami (tr. Audibert, J.-M. & Flak, M.). *Méditations tantriques: avec des transcriptions de séances*. - **[@satyanandasaraswati2021]** Satyananda Saraswati, Swami (tr. Prakashananda Saraswati, Swami). *Meditationen aus den Tantras*. Snippets read via the legacy Google Books feed; the page view was rate-limited. ToC scan: https://d-nb.info/966320271/04 (yoga chapter pp. 350-359).
- **[@schultz1935]** Schultz, J. H. *Übungsheft für das autogene Training (konzentrative Selbstentspannung); now 'Autogenes Training: das Original-Übungsheft'*. Thieme, Leipzig; 27th ed. TRIAS 2020 (96 pp., ISBN 978-3-432-11035-6). 1935. <https://d-nb.info/1289691711/04> (r4, confirmed, practitioner, de) — Practical booklet with the six standard exercises (heaviness, warmth, heart, breathing, solar plexus, head). The 1935 ToC was read; the 2004 edition has a partial Google Books preview (bpV-xMxnVMkC) with the formula wording.
- **[@schultz1959]** Schultz, J. H. *Autogenic training*. not checked (reportedly prepared with W. Luthe). 1959. <https://openlibrary.org/works/OL7073533W> (r3, *unverified*, historical) — Open Library listing only. This is the first English-language version; check the publisher and Luthe's role.
- **[@schultz1958]** Schultz, J. H. *Le training autogène: méthode de relaxation par auto-décontraction concentrative*. Presses universitaires de France. 1958. <https://archive.org/details/letrainingautogn0000joha> (r4, read, historical, fr) — French translation (Durand de Bousingen). A 2001 PUF printing (ISBN 9782130443124) is on Internet Archive controlled lending; only the metadata was checked. Relevant to the sophrology comparison (11 §11.2).
- **[@schultz1932]** Schultz, J.H. *Das autogene Training: konzentrative Selbstentspannung*. Thieme, Leipzig. 1932. <https://openlibrary.org/works/OL7073534W> (r4, confirmed, historical/scholarly/cultural, de, cited ×6) — First ed. 1932; Open Library work lists later editions (1964). Autogenic training grew out of Schultz's hypnosis research
- **[@sesma2022]** Sesma Nuez, A. *Yoga-nidra*. Kairós, 352 pp. 2022. <https://openlibrary.org/works/OL26586023W> (r3, confirmed, practitioner/cultural, es, cited ×3) — Spanish; via compiler discover-style Open Library query
- **[@sesma2024]** Sesma Nuez, A. *Doce viajes. Yoga-Nidra*. Publisher press kit gives release as 1st half of Nov 2023 (candidates.json says 2024). Goodreads 4.0 from 2 ratings.
- **[@shafer2024]** Shafer, K. *Falling Awake: The FUN® Guide to Integrating EMDR, Brainspotting and Yoga Nidra for Trauma and Self-Regulation*. The yoga nidra content (Ch. 5 pp. 99-119, App. C-D pp. 189-207) follows I AM Yoga Nidra (Kamini Desai co-wrote the script and wrote the foreword), not iRest. Publisher date 21 Nov 2024; the copyright page reads first published 2025. The pre-publication subtitle 'Integrating the Mindbody Therapies of...' is superseded. Contains unsupported claims (45 min = 3 h sleep, p. 110; delta/brain-injury speculation, p. 104).
- **[@shankardevananda1978]** Shankardevananda Saraswati, Swami. *The Effects of Yoga on Hypertension*. Bihar School of Yoga, Monghyr. 1978. (r2, confirmed, practitioner/historical)

- **[@singleton2010]** Singleton, M. *Yoga Body: The Origins of Modern Posture Practice*. Oxford University Press. 2010. <https://openlibrary.org/works/OL13775349W> (r5, confirmed, scholarly/historical, cited ×3)
- **[@sivananda1973]** Sivananda, S. *Yoga de la Kundalini (French translation of Kundalini Yoga)*. Épi, Paris (tr. - **[@skuban2012]** Skuban, R. *Yoga Nidra: Tiefenentspannung und geistige Klarheit (mit Übungs-CD)*. TOC seen (DNB scan https://d-nb.info/1021809675/04). Himalayan-tradition (Swami Rama) style sequence: body scan, cleansing breath, breath at energy centres, rest in heart centre; explicitly no sankalpa, no visualisation. Mandukya Upanishad appendix. Out of print; superseded by the 2023 Aquamarin edition.
- **[@skuban2023]** Skuban, R. *Yoga-Nidra: Tiefenentspannung, geistige Klarheit und Regeneration*. Aquamarin Verlag, Grafing. 2023. <https://aquamarin-verlag.de/medien/1357> (r3, read, practitioner, de) — Revised edition of Skuban 2012. Print ISBN 978-3-89427-922-6 (139 pp. DNB / 144 publisher); e-book ISBN 978-3-96861-304-8 (180 pp.) with Google Books preview id YZ3REAAAQBAJ. TOC seen (https://d-nb.info/1283972794/04). CD replaced by password-protected download from author's site; new preface; main text apparently lightly revised (page positions nearly unchanged).
- **[@skuban2025]** Skuban, R. *Jóga nidrá: hluboké uvolnění, duševní čistota a regenerace*. Subtitle suggests it translates the 2023 edition.
- **[@skyhawk2022]** Skyhawk, T. *Yoga Nidra-Meditationen: 22 Meditationen für mühelose Entspannung, Verjüngung und Wiederverbindung*. ISBN 978-1-990622-08-3, 12 Oct 2022 (Google Books). Translator not seen. Relevant for the German-market list in compendium ch. 11.
- **[@stanley2022]** Stanley, T. *Dormir consciente: Nidra yoga para la relajación profunda y el despertar de la lucidez*. - **[@stanley2021]** Stanley, Tracee. *Radiant Rest: Yoga Nidra for Deep Relaxation and Awakened Clarity*. Shambhala. 2021. <https://openlibrary.org/works/OL21926632W> (r3, confirmed, practitioner/cultural, cited ×11)
- **[@stanley2023]** Stanley, Tracee. *The Luminous Self: Sacred Yogic Practices and Rituals to Remember Who You Are*. - **[@strauss2005]** Strauss, S. *Positioning Yoga: Balancing Acts Across Cultures*. OL first-publish year 2004
- **[@stryker2011]** Stryker, R. *The Four Desires: Creating a Life of Purpose, Happiness, Prosperity, and Freedom*. Delacorte Press (Random House); UK: Hay House 2012. 2011. <https://openlibrary.org/books/OL25030584M> (r3, confirmed, practitioner, cited ×1) — Currently in rejected.json because 'its sankalpa method is restated in Enlightened Sleep', but Enlightened Sleep appears unpublished, so this is the only print source for Stryker's yoga nidra and sankalpa teaching. Part titles (Open Library): Life is desire; Live your mighty purpose; An intention formed in the heart; Overcoming resistance; Freedom from fear; The secret to success. 327 pp. No Google Books preview. Recommend reinstating it as stryker2011.
- **[@stuerzebecher]** Stürzebecher, N. *Yoga-Nidrā: Ascent into the Subconscious. Its roots, traditional foundations and modern developments: A comprehensive guide to the method developed by Svāmī Satyānanda Sarasvatī*. Purple Cloud Press (Purple Cloud Institute); pbk ISBN 978-1-991081-21-6, hbk ISBN 978-1-991081-22-3; 532 pp.; printed via Amazon KDP. 2026. <https://purplecloudinstitute.com/product/yoga-nidra-ascent-into-the-subconscious/> (r3, read, practitioner, cited ×1) — Released 22 Jan 2026. Written in German (completed 2023, German version unpublished), so this English edition is the first publication. Author's sannyasa name: Antarśuddhi; she is a BSY-authorised Satyananda Yoga Nidra trainer in Vienna. Academic consultant Georgi Krastev. Four parts plus Appendix: philosophy (Vedānta, Sāṃkhya, Śaiva Tantra, rāja-yoga, pratyāhāra, antaḥkaraṇa, pañcakośa, Māṇḍūkya, brainwaves), the eight stages, effects and research, secularised Western adaptations; the Appendix has a systematic literature review and a glossary. Deliberately no scripts; 3 downloadable audio practices. Ananda Verlag (Cologne) only retails it (EUR 44); it is not the publisher. Read: publisher sample pp. 13-17, 21-22, 27-31 and the TOC for ch. 1-10.

- **[@saraswati2005a]** Swami Satyananda Saraswati. *Yoga Nidra (German translation by Swami Prakashananda Saraswati)*. Yoga Publications Trust, Munger / Ananda Verlag. 2005 (3. Aufl.); 2019 (5. Aufl.); 2023 (6. Aufl.). <https://services.dnb.de/sru/dnb?version=1.1&operation=searchRetrieve&query=per%3DSatyananda%20and%20tit%3D%22Yoga%20Nidra%22&recordSchema=MARC21-xml> (r4, read, de) — Catalogue records only (DNB). 3rd ed. 2005, 279 pp., ISBN 978-3-928831-25-3, translated from English; 5th ed. 2019, 279 pp., ISBN 978-3-947908-05-9; 6th ed. 2023, viii+263 pp., ISBN 978-3-947908-25-7. - **[@thompson2015]** Thompson, E. *Waking, Dreaming, Being: Self and Consciousness in Neuroscience, Meditation, and Philosophy*. Hb ISBN 978-0-231-13709-6, pb 978-0-231-13695-2 (2017), e-book 978-0-231-53831-2; xl + 453 pp.; DOI 10.7312/thom13709. Ch. 8 (pp. 231-272) asks whether we are conscious in deep sleep.
