## Articles

- **[@akhandjyoti1983]** Akhand Jyoti (All World Gayatri Pariwar). *Yoga nidrā: viśrānti ke sāth pūrī nīnd kā lābh*. 1983. <https://www.awgp.org/en/literature/akhandjyoti/1983/November/v2.37> (r2, read, cultural/popular/historical, hi, cited ×8) — Read via fetch (English summary of the Hindi text). Hindi monthly: yoga nidra gives 'substantial vitality in little time', links it to pineal/pituitary, but says ordinary sleep is still needed. An early Indian instance of the sleep-substitute claim
- **[@amritanandasaraswati1982]** Amritananda Saraswati, Swami. *Yoga Nidra Class (Torino, 12 April 1981)*. Bihar School of Yoga, YOGA magazine April 1982. 1982. <http://www.yogamag.net/archives/1980s/1982/8204/8204ync.html> (r3, confirmed, practitioner) — A complete, freely readable Bihar-lineage class script: preparation, navel–throat breath, sankalpa 'always fulfilled', right-thumb rotation, opposites, ending with Om. Useful for comparing wording with the book's scripts.
- **[@weintraub2013]** Amy Weintraub. *Yoga Teacher Magazine interviews Amy Weintraub (2013; lineage, supine flooding risk, yoga nidra for grieving teachers)*. 2013. <https://yogafordepression.com/yoga-teacher-magazine-interviews-amy-weintraub/> (r2, confirmed)
- **[@weintraub2010]** Amy Weintraub. *Take a Breath: Using yoga to create a sense of well-being in your office (Psychotherapy Networker, reposted 2010)*. 2010. <https://yogafordepression.com/take-a-breath-using-yoga-in-your-office/> (r2, confirmed)
- **[@press1997]** Associated Press. *$1.9 Million Awarded In Swami Sexual Case*. The New York Times. 1997. <https://www.nytimes.com/1997/09/06/us/1.9-million-awarded-in-swami-sexual-case.html> (r4, *unverified*, critical/historical) — Not opened (NYT blocked the fetch); known only through Wikipedia's Swami Rama article. Verify before citing.
- **[@aznar2022]** Aznar, G. *Ana Sesma: «La finalidad última del yoga es descubrir quién soy yo»*. Diari de Tarragona (6 Jan 2022). 2022. <https://www.diaridetarragona.com/reus/Ana-Sesma-La-finalidad-ultima-del-yoga-es-descubrir-quien-soy-yo-20220106-0053.html> (r2, read, popular, es) — Local newspaper interview: contraindications (epilepsy, drug use, severe psychiatric conditions), the 'five windows' image, eleven-month stress leave as her route into yoga. Read via a fetch summary (partly paywalled).
- **[@beary1974]** Beary, J. F.; Benson, H. *A simple psychophysiologic technique which elicits the hypometabolic changes of the relaxation response*. Psychosomatic Medicine 36(2):115-120. 1974. <https://doi.org/10.1097/00006842-197403000-00003> (r4, confirmed, scholarly) — Abstract read: O2 consumption, CO2 production, respiratory rate fall; elements = mental device, passive attitude, decreased muscle tonus, quiet environment; sitting quietly failed to produce the same changes.
- **[@bechtel2012]** Bechtel, L. *Healing Trauma's Heart: An Interview with Richard Miller, founder of iRest*. Elephant Journal. 2012. <https://www.elephantjournal.com/2012/11/healing-traumas-heart-an-interview-with-richard-miller-founder-of-irest/> (r2, confirmed, popular) — 14 Nov 2012. Miller on sankalpa and heartfelt desire (with a non-standard etymology), the inner resource, bottom-up healing, and pacing so people are not overwhelmed by sensation. The introduction says Eric Schoomaker as Army Surgeon General endorsed yoga nidra as CAM for chronic pain in 2010.
- **[@benson1998]** Benson, H. *Staying Healthy in a Stressful World: Dr. Herbert Benson*. PBS Body & Soul / Beacon Productions. 1998. <https://www.pbs.org/bodyandsoul/218/benson.htm> (r3, confirmed, practitioner) — Benson in his own words: relaxation response as counterpart of the stress response, 'remembered wellness' (placebo), the role of belief and faith.
- **[@benson1978]** Benson, H.; Frankel, F. H.; Apfel, R.; et al. *Treatment of anxiety: a comparison of the usefulness of self-hypnosis and a meditational relaxation technique*. Psychotherapy and Psychosomatics 30(3-4):229-242. 1978. <https://doi.org/10.1159/000287304> (r4, confirmed, scholarly) — Abstract read: meditation vs self-hypnosis no difference for anxiety; hypnotic responsivity mattered more than technique. Relevant to the 'is it hypnosis?' debate.
- **[@benson1975a]** Benson, H.; Greenwood, M. M.; Klemchuk, H. *The relaxation response: psychophysiologic aspects and clinical applications*. International Journal of Psychiatry in Medicine 6(1-2):87-98. 1975. <https://doi.org/10.2190/376W-E4MT-QM6Q-H0UM> (r4, confirmed, scholarly) — Abstract read: relaxation response as an integrated hypothalamic response, counterpart of the emergency reaction.
- **[@bhushan2001]** Bhushan, S. *Yoga Nidra: Its Advantages and Applications*. Bihar School of Yoga, YOGA magazine March 2001. 2001. <http://www.yogamag.net/archives/2000s/2001/0103/0103ygnd.html> (r3, confirmed, practitioner/scholarly) — A lecturer at Bihar Yoga Bharati quotes the 6th edition (1998): the hour equal to four hours of sleep, the sankalpa as seed and irrigation, 'never fails', doctors prescribing it. Lists the 8 stages and the standard rotation order. Cites research pieces printed inside the 6th edition (Shealy on insomnia; Mangalteertham/Ghosh on altered states). Makes cancer-cure claims. A good window into the book's research section.
- **[@yoga1978]** Bihar School of Yoga. *Editorial [YOGA, August 1978: compilation of lectures and classes on yoga nidra]*. Bihar School of Yoga, YOGA magazine (online archive yogamag.net). 1978. <http://www.yogamag.net/archives/1970s/1978/7808/7808ed.html> (r4, confirmed, official/historical/practitioner, cited ×6) — Opens an issue the editors call a compilation of Satyananda's lectures and classes 'of the last eight years', divided into theory and practice. The issue refers to practices 'in this book', so it closely parallels the book of the time. Says Satyananda found the technique while revising tantric texts and began using it in 1962. Glosses nidra as 'relaxation'. States that one hour of yoga nidra in the day equals four hours of night sleep. Cites Bulgarian accelerated-language-learning experiments.
- **[@yoga1978a]** Bihar School of Yoga. *Yoga Nidra [YOGA, August 1978 compilation]*. Bihar School of Yoga, YOGA magazine (yogamag.net). 1978. <http://www.yogamag.net/archives/1970s/1978/7808/7808yn.html> (r4, confirmed, official/practitioner/historical, cited ×4) — The fullest free statement of the Satyananda method's logic: threefold tension; stages; about 76 rotation points; pratyahara reasoning; rapid images and chidakasha; the golden egg for dharana; sankalpa rules with future-tense 'I will...' examples (not present tense).
- **[@yoga1978b]** Bihar School of Yoga. *Preparing for Yoga Nidra [YOGA, August 1978 compilation]*. Bihar School of Yoga, YOGA magazine (yogamag.net). 1978. <http://www.yogamag.net/archives/1970s/1978/7808/7808pryn.html> (r4, confirmed, official/practitioner) — Room, timing, shavasana and its adaptations, preparatory asanas. On sleep: if you fall asleep your body needs it. Pacing (first rotation fast; vary length by whole stages). Warnings: gradual ending; phobia-triggering images; never invalidate experiences; avoid saying 'don't worry if you don't see this'.
- **[@yoga1978c]** Bihar School of Yoga. *Hypnotism [YOGA, August 1978 compilation]*. Bihar School of Yoga, YOGA magazine (yogamag.net). 1978. <http://www.yogamag.net/archives/1970s/1978/7808/7808hypn.html> (r4, confirmed, official/practitioner) — The lineage's own comparison of yoga nidra and hypnotherapy. Admits the instructions are given 'authoritatively and directively' but says the only therapeutic, suggestive content is the practitioner's own resolve. Useful for debate 10 ('Is it hypnosis?').
- **[@yoga1978d]** Bihar School of Yoga. *Psychological Aspects [YOGA, August 1978 compilation]*. Bihar School of Yoga, YOGA magazine (yogamag.net). 1978. <http://www.yogamag.net/archives/1970s/1978/7808/7808psya.html> (r3, confirmed, official/historical) — Freud's iceberg and ego-as-censor, Jung's collective unconscious, 'self-induced dreaming'. Claims relaxation deeper than sleep, plus 1970s physiology (lactate, alpha). The source of the 'subconscious/unconscious' explanation.
- **[@yoga1978e]** Bihar School of Yoga. *Yoga and the Mind [YOGA, August 1978 compilation]*. Bihar School of Yoga, YOGA magazine (yogamag.net). 1978. <http://www.yogamag.net/archives/1970s/1978/7808/7808ygmi.html> (r3, confirmed, official/practitioner) — Places yoga nidra in raja yoga as pratyahara leading to dharana and dhyana. Explains the psychic symbol and samskara images (frightening images giving way to peaceful ones) and the witnessing attitude.
- **[@unsigned1998]** Bihar School of Yoga (unsigned; childhood remark matches one attributed to Niranjanananda Saraswati, Swami). *Contribution of the Satyananda Tradition to Yoga*. YOGA magazine (Bihar School of Yoga), July 1998. 1998. <http://www.yogamag.net/archives/1990s/1998/9807/9807cont.html> (r3, confirmed, official/practitioner) — Lineage self-account: Satyananda incorporated tantric nyasa into yoga as yoga nidra; tantric nyasa meditation techniques were 'presented in their basic form' in Meditations from the Tantras; yoga nidra classed as a pratyahara technique.
- **[@birch2015]** Birch, Jason & Hargreaves, Jacqueline. *Yoganidrā: An Understanding of the History and Context*. The Luminescent. 2015. <https://www.theluminescent.org/2015/01/yoganidra.html> (r4, read, scholarly/historical, cited ×29) — Philological history of the term: epics, Puranas, Saiva and Buddhist tantras, medieval hatha/rajayoga texts; a posture name by the 17th c.
- **[@boehle2012]** Boehle, M. *Anna Röcker: 'Die Wahrheit des Yoga wird überdauern'*. Yoga Journal (German edition), pp. 94-97. 2012. <https://www.annaroecker.de/wp-content/uploads/2018/10/Yoga-Journal-11-12-2012.pdf> (r2, confirmed, practitioner, de) — Sidebar lists Röcker's 7 stages of yoga nidra, matching the 7-step programme on her 2007 CD. Gives music criteria for induction (Baroque/early Classical, slower than the heartbeat) and her origin account (Tantra, Patanjali's pratyahara, Satyananda). Describes yoga nidra as therapy homework. Her press page lists it as issue 4/2012; the PDF running header reads 06/2012.
- **[@bonnasse]** Bonnasse, P. *Yoga Nidra: The Yoga of Conscious Sleep (excerpt, 'The Ultimate Surrender')*. Light of Consciousness journal (spring issue). <https://www.nidra-yoga.com/excerpt-pierre-bonnasse-light-consciousness/> (r3, read, practitioner) — Same text as the publisher's excerpt, reprinted on the author's site with the full English TOC including session titles. Year of the journal issue not given.
- **[@bonnasse2022]** Bonnasse, P. *Yoganidrā & la fête du sommeil (Śayana-Utsava)*. Rishi Yoga Shala (médiathèque). 2022. <https://www.rishiyogashala.com/mediatheque/yn-fete-sommeil-article-pb/> (r3, confirmed, cultural, fr) — Short article on Viṣṇu's four-month sleep in the goddess Yoganidrā (Śayana-ekādaśī to the waking festival in Kārttika), read as a yogic resolve. Shows the author's later goddess-centred framing.
- **[@bonnasse2019a]** Bonnasse, P. *L'acceptation joyeuse (carte blanche)*. Esprit Yoga no. 50 (July 2019), p. 82. 2019. <https://www.pierrebonnasse.com/biblio-articles-entretiens/l-acceptation-joyeuse/> (r2, confirmed, practitioner/popular, fr) — One-page essay on non-dual acceptance, published at the book's release with a 'dernier ouvrage paru' box. Names three favourite books (Chinmayananda's Gita, Anirvan's Antara Yoga, a Ramana book). Scan on the author's site; the only item in the publisher's 'media' tab.
- **[@bonnasse2018]** Bonnasse, P. *Le pèlerinage dans le cadavre (śava-yātrā)*. Infos-Yoga no. 119 (dossier Yoga-Nidra), Oct–Nov 2018. 2018. <https://www.pierrebonnasse.com/publications-de-pierre-bonnasse/> (r2, *unverified*, practitioner, fr) — Seen only in the author's bibliography; not online. Written while the 2019 book was in preparation. Suggests śavayātrā (Himalayan tradition) is among his techniques.
- **[@boyes1984]** Boyes, D. *Aspects psychologiques et spirituels de la relaxation*. Revue 3e Millénaire (ancienne série) no. 14; republished on the magazine's site. 1984. <https://www.revue3emillenaire.com/blog/aspects-psychologiques-spirituels-de-relaxation-dennis-boyes/> (r3, read, practitioner, fr) — Part 1 of Boyes's own 4-part series. Read in full. Body as sensation; moves from coarse to fine sensations, then emptiness; warns against accentuating heaviness (anti-autogenic); esoteric somatisation theory. Lists his books to 1984.
- **[@boyes1984a]** Boyes, D. *La relaxation se transforme en méditation*. Revue 3e Millénaire (ancienne série) no. 15, July-Aug. 1984. 1984. <https://www.revue3emillenaire.com/blog/relaxation-se-transforme-meditation-dennis-boyes/> (r3, read, practitioner, fr) — Read in full. Written by Boyes himself with an editorial intro. Wikipedia wrongly calls it an anonymous review. Relaxation turns into meditation at emptiness; don't label or verbalise sensations; Krishnamurti-like social critique.
- **[@boyes1984b]** Boyes, D. *L'importance de l'observation*. Revue 3e Millénaire (ancienne série) no. 16, Sept.-Oct. 1984. 1984. <https://www.revue3emillenaire.com/blog/limportance-de-lobservation-par-dennis-boyes/> (r3, read, practitioner, fr) — Read in full. Rationale for touring every body part (unconscious emotions lodge in unconscious parts). Penetrating vs panoramic attention; sleep is the main obstacle in panoramic attention. Wanting results causes agitation and insomnia. Return through hearing, then touch, then sight. Consciousness continuous across the three states.
- **[@boyes1984c]** Boyes, D. *Aspects psychologiques et spirituels de la relaxation (fin): différences entre les méthodes occidentales et orientales*. Revue 3e Millénaire (ancienne série) no. 17, Nov.-Dec. 1984. 1984. <https://www.revue3emillenaire.com/blog/aspects-psychologiques-et-spirituels-de-la-relaxation-fin-par-dennis-boyes/> (r3, read, practitioner, fr) — Read in full. Argues that Western relaxation uses suggestion and authentic Eastern methods don't: suggestion aims at a future self, creates conflict and strengthens the ego; images and concepts reinforce name-and-form. An early argument against sankalpa-style resolves (debate 08 §8, §10).
- **[@wroth2013]** Carmel Wroth. *Yoga Journal interviews Amy Weintraub (Carmel Wroth, May 2013)*. 2013. <https://yogafordepression.com/yoga-journal-interviews-amy-weintraub/> (r2, confirmed)
- **[@costa2018]** Costa, Dora L.; Yetter, Noelle; DeSomer, Heather. *Intergenerational transmission of paternal trauma among US Civil War ex-POWs*. Proceedings of the National Academy of Sciences 115(44):11215-11220. 2018. <https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/30322945/> (r5, confirmed) — Abstract read via PubMed E-utilities; doi 10.1073/pnas.1803630115. Likely the study Stanley cites p. 60.
- **[@couture1999]** Couture, A. *The Problem of the Meaning of Yoganidrā's Name*. Journal of Indian Philosophy 27(1-2): 35-47. 1999. <https://doi.org/10.1023/A:1004345607620> (r4, confirmed, scholarly/historical) — Thompson cites it for the history of the term. Metadata checked on Crossref; the article itself was not read. A candidate for the history chapter alongside Birch & Hargreaves 2015.
- **[@desai2017a]** Desai, K. *The 5 Koshas: A Guide to Understanding Your Energy Bodies (excerpt from Yoga Nidra: The Art of Transformational Sleep)*. Conscious Lifestyle Magazine. 2017. <https://www.consciouslifestylemag.com/five-koshas-energy-sheath/> (r3, confirmed, practitioner) — Excerpt printed with the publisher's permission from the book (probably Ch. 5). Gives the kosha ladder ice/water/steam/air/ether; the Bliss/causal body holds chitta and samskaras, including past-life imprints; yoga nidra rewrites them; the Amrit method focuses on the Energy body. The only substantial book text available free and legitimately.
- **[@desai2026]** Desai, K. *How to Apply Sankalpa Practice in Daily Life*. kaminidesai.com. 2026. <https://www.kaminidesai.com/single-post/how-to-apply-sankalpa-practice-in-daily-life> (r2, confirmed, practitioner) — Sept 2026 post in a high-frequency, SEO-style blog series (authorship may be delegated). Trauma-aware sankalpa advice: believable wording, keep one for weeks, repeat 3 times at the start and end, step back if distressed. Not evidence for what the 2017 book says.
- **[@desaiapps]** Desai, Kamini. *7 Best Yoga Nidra Apps for Deep Rest*. <https://www.kaminidesai.com/single-post/7-best-yoga-nidra-apps-for-deep-rest> (r2, read, industry/popular, cited ×2) — App overview (Insight Timer, Calm, Headspace, iRest, BetterSleep, Simple Habit); conflict of interest: promotes author's own app. Year not shown
- **[@descamps2005]** Descamps, Marc-Alain. *Yoga-nidra ou la visualisation en relaxation profonde*. europsy.org (author's website, Internet Archive snapshot). 2005. <https://web.archive.org/web/20050220151057/http://www.europsy.org:80/marc-alain/yn.html> (r3, confirmed, fr) — Author's own article; precursor to his 2004 booklet Yoga-Nidra and likely to the 2015 book. Twilight/alpha state, 1–2 h sessions, inserted images/suggestopedia, critique of Western 'mental' yoga nidra; names Devatmananda, Boyes, Bhaktananda as early Western students of Satyananda c.1968.
- **[@dinsmoretuli2022a]** Dinsmore-Tuli, U. *Generous Abundance (a celebratory pilgrimage through the realms of Nidrā Shakti, Queen of Rest and Dreams)*. Kith Review, Issue 1 'Abundance', Autumn 2022, pp. 20-36 (Cinnamon Press). 2022. <https://www.yoganidranetwork.org/wp-content/uploads/2023/01/NIDRA-SHAKTI-in-KITH-LITERATRY-REVIEW.pdf> (r3, confirmed, practitioner) — Pre-publication excerpts from Nidra Shakti, hosted by the author. Contains: 108 praises of Nidra Shakti; a labyrinth diagram of the components; section headings that follow the Total Yoga Nidra stages; the Vijnana Bhairava Tantra v.75; the claim of 13 named yoga nidra methods; a celebratory, uncritical account of the research (internally inconsistent counts of 84 studies); and an author's postscript on post-lineage, improvised practice. Dates the Devi Mahatmya to 'c600 BC' (questionable). Scanned image PDF, no text layer.
- **[@dinsmoretuli2019]** Dinsmore-Tuli, U. *On Post-Lineage Yoga (Perspective, as told to Laurie Hyland Robertson)*. Yoga Therapy Today (IAYT), Spring 2019, pp. 46-48. 2019. <https://www.yoganidranetwork.org/wp-content/uploads/2022/07/YTT_Spring2019_Dinsmore-Tuli.pdf> (r3, confirmed, practitioner) — The author's account of the post-lineage stance (credits Theodora Wildcroft's PhD research). Uses the 'active ingredient in the box / trademarked Method on the outside' image for yoga nidra. Mentions the Avalon yoga camp. Written while Nidra Shakti was in progress.
- **[@dinsmoretuli2022e]** Dinsmore-Tuli, U. & Tuli, N. *Welcome to your best rest! (Yoga nidra feature, Psychologies UK, July 2022)*. Psychologies (Kelsey Media); PDF hosted by Yoga Nidrā Network. 2022. <https://www.yoganidranetwork.org/wp-content/uploads/2022/08/Psychologies-July22.pdf> (r3, confirmed, popular) — 5-page magazine excerpt of the book's introduction and the complete nine-minute rhythmic practice 'Welcome home to your rested self'. Free, legitimate excerpt.
- **[@dusek2008]** Dusek, J. A.; Otu, H. H.; Wohlhueter, A. L.; et al. *Genomic counter-stress changes induced by the relaxation response*. PLoS ONE 3(7):e2576. 2008. <https://doi.org/10.1371/journal.pone.0002576> (r3, confirmed, scholarly) — Abstract read: Benson's group's later gene-expression claims; defines RR markers as decreased O2 consumption, increased exhaled NO, reduced distress. Erratum 2017.
- **[@erikagreve]** Erika Greve, Natalie Backman. *Yoga Nidra: Deep Healing for Body, Mind & Heart, an interview with Yogarupa Rod Stryker by Erika Greve & Natalie Backman*. ParaYoga. <https://www.parayoga.com/yoga-nidra-deep-healing-for-body-mind-heart-an-interview-with-yogarupa-rod-stryker-by-erika-greve-natalie-backman/> (r3, *unverified*) — Redirects to a 404 on rodstryker.com; a Wayback snapshot exists (2024-11-12) but could not be reached from here. Not used; worth reading for his guiding style.
- **[@thompson2014a]** Evan Thompson. *Waking, Dreaming, Being (author post, crossposted from HuffPost)*. Columbia University Press Blog. 2014. <https://cupblog.org/2014/11/13/waking-dreaming-being/> (r4, confirmed) — Thompson describes his lifelong habit of watching for the onset of sleep, the Dharamsala lucid dream behind the book, and the hypnagogic loosening of self.
- **[@zutshi]** Evan Thompson / Vikram Zutshi. *Waking, Dreaming, Being (interview with Vikram Zutshi)*. Science and Nonduality (SAND). <https://scienceandnonduality.com/article/waking-dreaming-being/> (r3, confirmed) — Author background; as a child he tried to watch his mind fall asleep.
- **[@ferrarelli2013]** Ferrarelli, F. *Experienced Mindfulness Meditators Exhibit Higher Parietal-Occipital EEG Gamma Activity during NREM Sleep*. PLoS ONE 8(8): e73417. 2013. <https://doi.org/10.1371/journal.pone.0073417> (r4, confirmed, scholarly) — Cited by Thompson as suggestive evidence that meditation alters NREM sleep (gamma correlates with years of practice). Metadata confirmed on Crossref; paper not opened. Not a yoga nidra study.
- **[@ganpat2011]** Ganpat, Tikhe Sham; Nagendra, H. R.; Muralidhar, K. *Effects of yoga on brain wave coherence in executives*. Indian J Physiol Pharmacol 55(4):304-308. 2011. <https://ijpp.com/IJPP%20archives/2011_55_4/304-308.pdf> (r2, confirmed) — 5-day SMET course, 72 ONGC executives, pre/post, no control group; only the delta coherence change was significant (p=0.03). Gives the SMET daily schedule (CM twice a day, MSRT).
- **[@green1974]** Green, E. *Biofeedback and Yoga*. The Five Minute Hour (Geigy Pharmaceuticals, Ardsley NY); reprinted in Subtle Energies & Energy Medicine 10(2):46-52. 1974. <https://elmergreenfoundation.org/wp-content/uploads/2018/12/Biofeedback-and-Yoga.pdf> (r3, confirmed, historical/practitioner, cited ×4) — The Greens' own first-hand account of Swami Rama's 25-minute 'yogic sleep' session (autumn 1970): snoring, scattered delta in a variable stage 2-4 record, recall of 3.5 of 4 sentences. Hedged wording; one session, no published polysomnography. The reprint's editor's note prints the year as '1914', a typo; the text cites a February 1974 meeting. Reprint volume year (c. 1999) unchecked. This, not Beyond Biofeedback, is the correct citation for the delta episode.
- **[@gurumaa2025]** Gurumaa, A. *Arresting & Busting Stress With Yoga Nidra (talk at Rishihood University, Sonipat)*. gurumaa.com. 2025. <https://www.gurumaa.com/anandmurti-gurumaa/teachings/yoga-nidra/busting-stress-yoga-nidra> (r1, confirmed, practitioner) — Author's own talk. Describes the ICMR study as a 'double-blind placebo trial over six months' with EEGs, which contradicts the published paper (non-randomised, unblinded, no placebo, 12 weeks). Also a school self-report of 21 students ('95.2% reduction of stress'), sessions at IIT Delhi, NSIT and Tihar Jail, and 'UN: stress the biggest killer'. Useful as an example of claim inflation.
- **[@heikkila2013]** Heikkilä, K. *Work stress and risk of cancer: meta-analysis of 5700 incident cancer events in 116,000 European men and women*. BMJ 346:f165. 2013. <https://doi.org/10.1136/bmj.f165> (r5, confirmed, scholarly) — PMID 23393080 / PMC3567204. Job strain was not associated with overall, colorectal, lung, breast or prostate cancer risk. Used to check yoga nidra books' stress-causes-cancer claims (Panda p. 238). Abstract read via Europe PMC.
- **[@hersey2022b]** Hersey, T. *Rest is anything that connects your mind and body*. The Nap Ministry blog. 2022. <https://thenapministry.wordpress.com/2022/02/21/rest-is-anything-that-connects-your-mind-and-body/> (r4, confirmed, critical) — 21 Feb 2022. 'This is about more than naps'. Dates the framework to 2016. Rejects consumerist sleep products. Calls anyone who promotes rest-as-resistance without its Black-liberation history an 'agent of grind culture'. Key source on attribution and co-optation.
- **[@hersey2021]** Hersey, T. *Our work is has a framework*. The Nap Ministry blog. 2021. <https://thenapministry.wordpress.com/2021/01/11/our-work-is-has-a-framework/> (r4, confirmed, official) — 11 Jan 2021. Names the influences: Black Liberation Theology (Cone), womanism (Layli Phillips), Afrofuturism, reparations theory, somatics, community organizing. States 'we have never identified ourselves as being a part of the wellness industry'.
- **[@hoffman2025]** Hoffman, K. *Intentions*. Substack. 2025. <https://katiehoffman.substack.com/p/intentions> (r1, read, community) — 28 Dec 2025. A secondhand mention that Desai's book calls intention 'reality-friendly' and lists five qualities of an effective intention; the qualities themselves are not given.
- **[@holmes1984]** Holmes, D. S. *Meditation and somatic arousal reduction: A review of the experimental evidence*. American Psychologist 39(1):1-10. 1984. <https://doi.org/10.1037/0003-066x.39.1.1> (r4, read, critical) — Standard critical review (meditation ~ rest). Record only; no abstract seen.
- **[@holmes1983]** Holmes, D. S.; Solomon, S.; Cappo, B. M.; Greenberg, J. L. *Effect of Transcendental Meditation versus resting on physiological and subjective arousal*. Journal of Personality and Social Psychology 44(6):1245-1252. 1983. <https://doi.org/10.1037/0022-3514.44.6.1245> (r4, confirmed, critical) — Abstract read: meditators while meditating were not less aroused than non-meditators resting. Key critique of the hypometabolic claim.
- **[@holzel2011]** Hölzel BK et al. *Mindfulness practice leads to increases in regional brain gray matter density*. Psychiatry Research: Neuroimaging 191:36-43. 2011. <https://doi.org/10.1016/j.pscychresns.2010.08.006> (r5, confirmed) — 8-week MBSR study, cited in Desai's book (figure p. 227); not a yoga nidra study. Metadata checked on Crossref.
- **[@janakanandasaraswati2016]** Janakananda Saraswati, Swami. *On the Ability to Experience*. Scandinavian Yoga and Meditation School (Bindu). n.d. (online 2016). <https://www.yogameditation.com/reading-room/on-the-ability-to-experience/> (r2, read, practitioner) — Same title as a section of the book. Contains strong anecdotal health claims (e.g., insulin production restarting during a meditation course).
- **[@priyashakti2008]** Julie Friedeberger (Priyashakti). *Yoga Nidra: A Healing Practice for People Living with Cancer*. YOGA magazine, Bihar School of Yoga. 2008. <http://www.yogamag.net/archives/2000s/2008/0804/0804yn.html> (r3, confirmed) — April 2008. Quotes the 6th edition of Satyananda's Yoga Nidra (cited as 1998) twice, on nadis and health and on yoga nidra and sankalpa as cancer therapy. No page numbers.
- **[@jyotirananda2022]** Jyotirananda, I. *Ventajas de Yoga Nidra Mindfulness Sistema Darshan respecto Yoga Nidra*. Centre de Teràpies Darshan (yogabarcelona.net). 2022. <https://www.yogabarcelona.net/es/ventajas-de-yoga-nidra-mindfulness-sistema-darshan-respecto-yoga-nidra/> (r2, read, practitioner, es) — Four claimed differences from 'original' (Satyananda) yoga nidra: no direct orders or relaxation commands (NLP indirect language), NLP-designed secret affirmation instead of a three-times sankalpa, no presupposed outcomes (lists of banned phrases), 5-20 min 'essential' versions; long version's structure 'almost identical' to Bihar.
- **[@jyotirananda2018]** Jyotirananda, I. *Entrevista Ignasi Jyotirananda, co-creador del Sistema Yoga Nidra Mindfulness Darshan*. Centre de Teràpies Darshan (yogabarcelona.net), self-published. 2018. <https://www.yogabarcelona.net/es/entrevista-ignasi-jyotirananda-co-creador-del-sistema-yoga-nidra-mindfulness-darshan-barcelona/> (r2, read, practitioner, es) — Self-published Q&A on the authors' blog, not an independent interview. How he discovered yoga nidra (mid-1990s), 6 months' training in India (Satyananda ashram) and Nepal (Alakh Shakti); structure called 'identical' to Bihar.
- **[@jyotirananda2021]** Jyotirananda, I.; Vidyanandi, M. *Afirmaciones Personales Samkalpa y Niveles PNL para yoga nidra mindfulness*. Centre de Teràpies Darshan (yogabarcelona.net). 2021. <https://www.yogabarcelona.net/es/afirmaciones-personales-samkalpa-y-niveles-pnl-para-yoga-nidra-mindfulness/> (r2, read, practitioner, es) — Sankalpa designed with the NLP 'logical levels' (environment, behaviour, capabilities, beliefs/values, identity, spiritual), kept secret, repeated in the 'alpha state'. Unsupported mechanism claims.
- **[@desai2026a]** Kamini Desai. *Yoga Nidra Facilitation That Creates Safety*. kaminidesai.com. 2026. <https://www.kaminidesai.com/single-post/yoga-nidra-facilitation-that-creates-safety> (r3, confirmed, practitioner) — Dated 7 Sep 2026 in the blog feed, which credits Kamini Desai as author. General facilitation advice, not a script: invitational over directive language, normalising position changes, opening the eyes or ending early, a generic practice arc and pacing suited to the group. Read in full.
- **[@kissner2016]** Kissner, S. *Nichts ist in Stein gemeißelt (portrait/interview with Anna Trökes)*. Yoga Journal (German edition), 01/2016, pp. 92-97. 2016. <https://prana-yogaschule.de/wp-content/uploads/2024/04/I_L_YJ_43_092_97.pdf> (r3, confirmed, practitioner, de) — Hosted on the author's site. Her biography and teachers (Gérard Blitz, Desikachar, Patrick Tomatis, Anneliese Harf, Roger Clerc's Yoga der Energie); BDY teacher-training reform from 1984. No mention of yoga nidra or Satyananda. Useful for German post-war yoga history.
- **[@krause2022]** Krause, R. *Yoga Sleep*. AHYMSIN. 2022. <https://www.ahymsin.org/yoga-sleep/> (r2, confirmed, practitioner/community) — A disciple's anecdotes about Swami Veda: 2 h sleep plus 2 h yoga nidra; an hour-long Italian lecture memorised overnight in yoga nidra; delta waves while conversing at IONS (second-hand via S. Parker). Hagiographic; unverified.
- **[@kremer2015]** Kremer, W. *The man who invented relaxation*. BBC News Magazine. 2015. <https://www.bbc.com/news/magazine-34714591> (r3, confirmed, popular) — Interviews Paul Lehrer (trained with Jacobson in 1973), Ayesha Nathoo and Suzanne Newcombe. Newcombe calls tense-and-release in shavasana a 'Western medical imposition', not in medieval yoga texts. Covers Lee Birk's 1966 curare experiment against Jacobson's thesis that thought is muscle activity, and the Jacobson-Schultz rivalry.
- **[@lehrer1994]** Lehrer, P. M.; Carr, R.; Sargunaraj, D.; Woolfolk, R. The main counter to Benson's single-mechanism thesis.
- **[@lorenceau2015]** Lorenceau, P. *Saveur du sommeil yogique (interview with Pierre Bonnasse by P. Lorenceau)*. Le Journal du Yoga, no. 160 (April 2015). 2015. <https://www.nidra-yoga.com/publications/articles-and-entretiens/> (r3, *unverified*, practitioner, fr) — Listed on the author's site; not opened. Probably the source of the English edition's appendix 'The Taste of Yogic Sleep' (inference from the matching title).
- **[@lusk2021a]** Lusk, J. T. *Yoga Nidra: Portal to Our Essence-Nature*. Integral Yoga Magazine. c. 2021. <https://integralyogamagazine.org/yoga-nidra-portal-to-our-essence-nature/> (r3, confirmed, practitioner) — Lusk's 'six levels' with labels mapped to annamaya through anandamaya plus pure awareness (Atman); about 30 minutes typical; lists techniques per level (PMR, homunculus/chakra/marma tour, autogenics, breath, visualization, mantra, opposites, witness). Written for the 2021 book.
- **[@markil2012]** Markil, N.; Whitehurst, M.; Jacobs, P. L.; Zoeller, R. F. *Yoga Nidra relaxation increases heart rate variability and is unaffected by a prior bout of Hatha yoga*. Example of yoga nidra research measuring Benson-type markers.
- **[@mason1997]** Mason, L. I. *Electrophysiological correlates of higher states of consciousness during sleep in long-term practitioners of the Transcendental Meditation program*. Sleep 20(2): 102-110. 1997. (r3, *unverified*, scholarly) — Cited by Thompson (Open MIND p. 16): TM long-termers reporting 'witnessing' sleep showed alpha/theta superimposed on delta in slow-wave sleep. Not yoga nidra and not opened. Relevant to the 06 section 6.3 claim of 'delta with awareness'.
- **[@mccammon2020]** McCammon, S. *Atlanta-Based Organization Advocates For Rest As A Form Of Social Justice*. NPR (Weekend Edition / transcript). 2020. <https://www.npr.org/2020/06/04/869952476/atlanta-based-organization-advocates-for-rest-as-a-form-of-social-justice> (r4, confirmed, popular) — Pre-book interview (4 June 2020): origin in divinity school around 2013, rest as healing and invention for Black liberation, sleep deprivation framed as a justice issue.
- **[@mcgonigal]** McGonigal, K. *How to Create a Sankalpa*. Yoga International (earlier version in Yoga Journal as 'Resolve to Evolve'). <https://yogainternational.com/article/view/how-to-create-a-sankalpa/> (r3, confirmed, practitioner) — Main accessible source for Rod Stryker's sankalpa method: kalpa gloss, the case against planting a resolve from lack, Tripura Rahasya, the ParaYoga 40-day 'departure point' practice, being/becoming (para/jiva atman). Also quotes Richard Miller (heartfelt desire; sravana/manana/nididhyasana; present tense) and ParaYoga teacher Brenna Geehan.
- **[@milian2021]** Milian, M. *El somni conscient*. El Vallenc (opinion column, 28 Nov 2021). 2021. <https://www.elvallenc.cat/opinio/opinio-montse-milian-el-somni-conscient/> (r1, read, popular, ca) — Local column recommending the book; repeats brainwave and serotonin claims; no criticism. Read via a fetch summary.
- **[@miller2014]** Miller, R. *The iRest Program for Healing Trauma*. The Shift Network (Catalyst, issue 7). 2014. <https://theshiftnetwork.com/blog/2014-04-04/irest-program-healing-trauma> (r2, read, practitioner) — Pre-publication article by Miller presenting 'ten essential tools'; cites a 2006 DoD-sponsored study leading to Walter Reed classes. Read via a fetch summary only.
- **[@miller2024]** Miller, R. *Find Lasting Peace (Yoga Journal meditation column, reposted)*. iRest Institute (originally Yoga Journal). 2024. <https://shop.irest.org/blogs/news/find-lasting-peace> (r2, confirmed, practitioner) — Ninth of Miller's 10 Yoga Journal columns. Explains sensations, emotions and thoughts as 'messengers' asking for action, and absorption in awareness 'much like deep, dreamless sleep'. Original Yoga Journal date not shown.
- **[@mira1999]** Mira. *Meditative Deep Relaxation (Yoga Nidra and consciousness)*. Scandinavian Yoga and Meditation School (Bindu / Reading Room). c. 1999 (Bindu no. 11). <https://www.yogameditation.com/reading-room/meditative-deep-relaxation/> (r3, confirmed, practitioner) — School's guidance on sleep (falling asleep OK at first, transitional; rejoin the instructions where they are), head to the north, firm surface, forearms-up trick to avoid dozing, fixed instructions so 'the subconscious feels secure', learn short version then self-guide.
- **[@moore2020a]** Moore, S. *Yoga Nidra: How Opposites Reveal Oneness*. Scott Moore Yoga (author's site). 2020. <https://www.scottmooreyoga.com/blog/yoga-nidra-how-opposites-reveal-oneness> (r3, confirmed, practitioner) — Key provenance statement: he took Richard Miller's iRest training first but says he does not teach that method. Explains his kosha-based, opposites-to-awareness technique and his 'Both And Nature' idea.
- **[@moore2020b]** Moore, S. *Yoga Nidra Script (free script: Basic Practice, Anamaya Kosha)*. Scott Moore Yoga (author's site). 2020. <https://www.scottmooreyoga.com/blog/yoga-nidra-script> (r3, confirmed, practitioner) — The author's full free 10-15 min script, contemporary with the book. Shows his intention formula, 'welcome, recognize, and witness' language, body sensing by region, large/small opposites, and a bell or countdown ending. Also advertises his paid '20 Yoga Nidra Scripts Vol. 1'.
- **[@moore2020c]** Moore, S. *The Many Paths of Yoga Nidra*. Scott Moore Yoga (author's site). 2020. <https://www.scottmooreyoga.com/blog/many-paths-of-yoga-nidra> (r3, confirmed, practitioner) — His definition: the goal is samadhi, reached via the hypnagogic state, systematic relaxation and layered awareness through the kosha 'layers of illusion'. Mentions 61 points and homunculus-based body scans. Non-dogmatic, teach-in-your-own-voice stance.
- **[@moore2024]** Moore, S. *How Long Should A Yoga Nidra Practice Be, Anyway?* Scott Moore Yoga (author's site). 2024. <https://www.scottmooreyoga.com/blog/2024/2/7/how-long-should-a-yoga-nidra-practice-be-anyway> (r2, confirmed, practitioner) — 30 min as his 'sweet spot', 15 min minimum, 45 max. Says it is OK to fall asleep. Outlines his 'Roadmap' (permissions, broad awareness, Sankalpa, Sanctuary, body scan, ..., review, affirmation). Contains an unsupported claim that 30 min of yoga nidra equals a 2-hour nap (cf. 06 §6.3). Attributes a 15-minute settling time to Judith Lasater.
- **[@muktanandasaraswati1979]** Muktananda Saraswati, Swami. *Yoga Nidra and the Brain, Parts 1–2*. Bihar School of Yoga, YOGA magazine January and February 1979. 1979. <http://www.yogamag.net/archives/1970s/1979/7901/7901ynb1.html> (r3, confirmed, practitioner/historical) — Part 2: http://www.yogamag.net/archives/1970s/1979/7902/7902ynb2.html. The Penfield homunculus rationale for the rotation, and why it starts at the right thumb (large cortical hand map, sensitivity). Probably the basis of the book's chapter of the same title (unconfirmed).
- **[@nilsson1999]** Nilsson, R. *Pictures of the brain's activity during Yoga Nidra*. Scandinavian Yoga and Meditation School (Bindu / Reading Room). c. 1999 (Bindu no. 11; web version updated after 2002). <https://www.yogameditation.com/reading-room/pictures-of-the-brains-activity-during-yoga-nidra/> (r3, confirmed, practitioner, cited ×1) — Read in full. School's account of Lou 1999/Kjaer 2002: subjects were the school's teachers listening to the Deep Yoga Nidra of Experience Yoga Nidra, did Kriya Yoga that morning; four scan points = body (esp. face), happiness/contentment, summer day in the countryside, 'who am I' at the end; EEG theta +11%, alpha -2% NS; 'seven yoga teachers' (abstract says nine). Overstates ('fourth major state' confirmed).
- **[@nilsson2016]** Nilsson, R. *Savasana – one of the most important yoga poses*. Scandinavian Yoga and Meditation School (Bindu). n.d. (online 2016). <https://www.yogameditation.com/reading-room/savasana/> (r3, confirmed, practitioner) — Distinguishes savasana (10-12 min of stillness) from yoga nidra, in which awareness is guided through the body, to the breath and to images at a 'brisk but calm' pace.
- **[@nilsson]** Nilsson, R. (compiler). *The relaxed State and Science*. Scandinavian Yoga and Meditation School (Reading Room). n.d.. <https://www.yogameditation.com/reading-room/the-relaxed-state-and-science/> (r2, confirmed, practitioner) — Quotes The Little Book on Yoga Nidra (the CD booklet). Reports an unpublished-looking early-1980s Cologne EEG comparison of yoga nidra vs suggestion-based relaxation; dated, uncritical list of relaxation-health studies; one line listing what the practice contains (body, breath, heaviness/lightness, warmth/cold, images, symbols).
- **[@nolan2025]** Nolan, B. *YOGA NIDRA: help us bring a book to life*. Field Notes From The Body (Substack). 2025. <https://beverleynolan.substack.com/p/yoga-nidra-help-us-bring-a-book-to> (r3, read, practitioner/community) — 12 Jan 2025. The book's aims (cut through misinformation and mystification; for yoga teachers, therapists, hypnotherapists); six live 'lab' sessions Feb-Jul 2025 with subscribers, covering NSDR and Somatic Dreaming.
- **[@nolan2024]** Nolan, B. *SLEEP: the keeper of mystery*. Field Notes From The Body (Substack). 2024. <https://beverleynolan.substack.com/p/sleep-the-keeper-of-mystery> (r2, read, practitioner/popular) — Pre-book essay on sleep physiology (SCN, melatonin, adenosine, chronotypes) and yoganidra; calls NSDR a secular label for yoganidra; lark/owl percentages given without sources. Useful for anticipating ch. 2's content and checking it.
- **[@pankhania2017]** Pankhania, J. & Hargreaves, J. *A Culture of Silence: Satyananda Yoga*. The Luminescent. 2017. <https://www.theluminescent.org/2017/12/a-culture-of-silence-satyananda-yoga.html> (r3, read, critical/scholarly, cited ×5) — Read via fetch (summary). Gives hearing Dec 2014, report 2016; argues institution unreformed
- **[@parker2011]** Parker, S. *A Successful Yoga Nidra Retreat (AHYMSIN Newsletter, May 2011)*. AHYMSIN. 2011. <https://www.ahymsin.org/docs2/News/1105May/01.html> (r3, confirmed, practitioner/official) — Report on the 2011 SRSG conference (250+ participants; Telles, Goel). A live EEG demo with one 40-year practitioner reportedly produced delta and recalled words read aloud; a pilot write-up was announced. Single subject, before an audience; I could not confirm that data was published.
- **[@peters1977]** Peters, R. K.; Benson, H.; Porter, D. *Daily relaxation response breaks in a working population: I. Effects on self-reported measures of health, performance, and well-being*. RR breaks > sitting quietly > no instruction; significance reported for RR vs no instruction.
- **[@reberammann2015]** Reber Ammann, K. *«Der Yoga ist eine Schatztruhe» (portrait of Anna Trökes)*. Swiss yoga magazine (title not shown in the PDF), Feb/Mar 2015, pp. 28-32. 2015. <https://prana-yogaschule.de/wp-content/uploads/2024/03/Schatztruhe-I_L_0115_28-32_Portraet_GzD.pdf> (r3, confirmed, practitioner, de) — Hosted on the author's site. Lists her teachers (Desikachar, Yesudian pupils, Harf, Werner Vogel, Roger Clerc, Nil Hahoutoff, Patrick Tomatis, Gérard Blitz) and the Zinal congresses. No mention of yoga nidra or Satyananda. The magazine's name should be verified.

Names the book's key sources: Birch & Hargreaves 2015, Singleton 2005, Parker et al. 2013 (with reservations), Roenneberg et al. 2007 on chronotypes; doubts about the popular biphasic-sleep story.
- **[@wildcroft2026c]** Wildcroft, T. *READ: How to write a book (again)*. Theo's Substack. 2026. <https://theowildcroft.substack.com/p/read-how-to-write-a-book-again> (r3, read, industry/practitioner) — Practical advice for yoga teachers writing a book (proposal contents, comparative titles, traditional vs self-publishing). Relevant to the user's own project; earlier version 2025: /p/read-how-to-write-a-book.
- **[@wildcroft2017]** Wildcroft, T. *The Launch and Reception of Roots of Yoga*. Open University, Religious Studies blog. 2017. <https://www.open.ac.uk/blogs/religious-studies/?p=405> (r3, read, scholarly) — Short blog post on the book's polarized reception along sectarian and political lines. Read only through a fetch-tool summary, since curl got a 403.
- **[@windt2016]** Windt, J. M. *Does Consciousness Disappear in Dreamless Sleep?* Trends in Cognitive Sciences. 2016. <https://doi.org/10.1016/j.tics.2016.09.006> (r4, read, scholarly) — With Nielsen & Thompson; PMID 27765517. Record only (not open access, not read). The follow-up review proposing a taxonomy of dreamless sleep experiences.
- **[@yogauonline2014]** YogaUOnline. *The Healing Power of Silence - Richard Miller on iRest Yoga Nidra*. YogaUOnline. 2014. <https://yogauonline.com/yoga-health-benefits/yoga-for-depression-and-anxiety/the-healing-power-of-silence-richard-miller-on-irest-yoga-nidra/> (r2, confirmed, popular) — 23 Sept 2014. Miller on the Inner Resource as a 'time out' before returning to difficult material; the military approached him in 2004; programmes at 35 VA centres and 6 DoD sites; announces the book (working title '...PTSD and Trauma') for November 2014, which explains the 2014/2015 date confusion. Contains a hippocampus-thickening claim that is the writer's paraphrase, not a finding about iRest.
- **[@staff2009]** YogaUOnline staff. *LifeForce Yoga for Depression*. YogaUOnline. 2009. <https://yogauonline.com/?p=15434> (r2, confirmed) — Says Weintraub recommends ending a LifeForce practice with a long yoga nidra relaxation.
- **[@ereau2022]** Éreau, R. *Yoga Nidra, l'éveil par le sommeil (dossier)*. Inexploré no. 55 (INREES), July–Sept 2022. 2022. <https://www.inexplore.com/magazines/Inexplore-55> (r2, read, popular, fr) — French popular-magazine feature ('Yoga Nidra – le sommeil lucide') with Bonnasse's participation, according to his site. Only the issue page and a one-line teaser were seen.

## Official documents

- **[@cyp]** Ministry of AYUSH, Government of India (ed. I.V. Basavaraddi, MDNIY). *Common Yoga Protocol (International Day of Yoga)*. 2015. <https://www.mea.gov.in/images/pdf/common-yoga-protocol-english.pdf> (r4, read, official/cultural, cited ×2) — Read PDF. 45-min state protocol; allows institutions 15 min of 'Institutional Yogic practices e.g. Prāṇāyāma, Yoga Nidrā, Dhyāna, Satsaṅg' before the closing sankalpa. Edition year approximate
- **[@mordor2026]** Mordor Intelligence. *Meditation Management Apps Market: Size, Share & Growth Trends Report 2026-2031*. 2026. <https://www.mordorintelligence.com/industry-reports/meditation-management-apps-market> (r2, read, industry/data, cited ×3) — Read via fetch (summary page only). USD 6.65 bn (2026) → 14.17 bn (2031); sleep/relaxation audio fastest-growing format (18.2% CAGR). Market-report estimates vary widely between vendors
- **[@nccih2021]** National Center for Complementary and Integrative Health (NIH). *Relaxation Techniques: What You Need To Know*. 2021. <https://www.nccih.nih.gov/health/relaxation-techniques-what-you-need-to-know> (r5, read, official, cited ×5) — Read via fetch. Evidence mostly low/very low; 'rare reports that certain relaxation techniques might cause or worsen symptoms in people with epilepsy or certain psychiatric conditions, or with a history of abuse or trauma'. Yoga nidra not listed separately
- **[@nccih2022]** National Center for Complementary and Integrative Health (NIH). *Meditation and Mindfulness: Effectiveness and Safety*. 2022. <https://www.nccih.nih.gov/health/meditation-and-mindfulness-effectiveness-and-safety> (r5, read, official, cited ×2) — Read via fetch. Cites 2020 review: 55 of 83 studies reported negative experiences; ~8% of participants
- **[@rc2016]** Royal Commission into Institutional Responses to Child Sexual Abuse (Australia). *Report of Case Study No. 21: The response of the Satyananda Yoga Ashram at Mangrove Mountain to allegations of child sexual abuse*. 2016. <https://www.childabuseroyalcommission.gov.au/case-studies/case-study-21-satyananda-yoga-ashram> (r5, confirmed, official/critical, cited ×6) — Page returned 503; findings PDF not downloadable from this environment. Findings as quoted in rfp2016 and pankhania2017: Satyananda had 'overarching authority'; no child-protection policy 1975-87/89; BSY response did not prioritise survivors over the 'brand'. Hearing Dec 2014; report release date given as April or 16 Sept 2016 by secondary sources
- **[@australia2016]** Royal Commission into Institutional Responses to Child Sexual Abuse (Australia). *Report into Satyananda Yoga Ashram released (Royal Commission media release, 14 September 2016), as reproduced on BishopAccountability.org*. BishopAccountability.org (Abuse Tracker excerpt). 2016. <https://www.bishop-accountability.org/2016/09/report-into-satyananda-yoga-ashram-released/> (r4, read, official/critical) — Gives the report release date of 14 Sept 2016, the public hearing of Dec 2014 and oral submissions of April 2015; concerns the Mangrove Mountain ashram and Akhandananda. The Commission's own site was unreachable.
- **[@vawholehealth]** U.S. Department of Veterans Affairs. *Meditation (Whole Health Library)*. <https://www.va.gov/WHOLEHEALTHLIBRARY/tools/meditation.asp> (r4, confirmed, official, cited ×1) — VA Whole Health clinician tool; via web search, not opened
- **[@vawriisc]** U.S. Department of Veterans Affairs, War Related Illness and Injury Study Center. *CA WRIISC Integrative Health and Wellness Program: iRest Yoga Nidra*. <https://www.warrelatedillness.va.gov/warrelatedillness/clinical/integrative-health/ca/index.asp> (r4, read, official, cited ×3) — Read via fetch: 10-stage iRest by telephone twice weekly for VA-enrolled veterans nationwide, by clinician referral
- **[@yogaaustralia]** Yoga Australia. *Yoga Australia Statement on the Royal Commission*. <https://www.yogaaustralia.org.au/royal-commission/> (r3, read, official, cited ×1) — Read via fetch. Professional body; links 2019 and 2022 statements; no concrete teacher guidance on the page

- **[@nationalbibliothek2014]** Deutsche Nationalbibliothek. *DNB catalogue record and scanned table of contents: Yoga Nidra : die Yoga-Tiefenentspannung ; inklusive CD (IDN 1047345943 / 1043918418)*. Deutsche Nationalbibliothek. 2014. <https://d-nb.info/1043918418/04> (r5, confirmed, data, de) — 1st ed. 2014, 80 pp., 20 cm; CD 70 min, 'Autorin und Sprecherin: Anna Trökes'; launch price EUR 16.99. TOC PDF matches the publisher sample. Publisher blurb at https://services.dnb.de/plus/idn/1047345943/blurb/

- **[@giridharan2026]** Giridharan, S. *Therapeutic Effects of Yoga Nidra on Human Health: An Umbrella Review of Systematic Reviews (OSF registration)*. 2026. <https://archive.org/details/osf-registrations-2hnr9-v1> (r3, confirmed, data/scholarly, cited ×1) — Pre-registration of an umbrella review, mirrored on archive.org; via compiler discover (archive)
- **[@ctri]** Indian Council of Medical Research, National Institute of Medical Statistics. *Clinical Trials Registry – India (CTRI)*. <https://ctri.nic.in/> (r4, confirmed, data/official/cultural, cited ×3) — Home page reachable; search is captcha-gated. Yoga nidra IDs found in published papers: CTRI/2013/05/003682 (datta2021), CTRI/2021/07/034521 (post-COVID), CTRI/2022/07/044426 (kumari2025), CTRI/2021/02/031349, CTRI/2023/01/048719
- **[@congress2025]** Library of Congress. *Library of Congress Name Authority: Paṇḍā, Nr̥siṃhacaraṇa, 1929- (n83179659)*. Library of Congress. 2025. <https://id.loc.gov/authorities/names/n83179659> (r5, confirmed, data) — Authorised name form and variants (N. C. Panda; Nrusingh/Nrusingha/Nursingha Charan Panda); born 20 April 1929; also an Odia novelist.
- **[@congress2015]** Library of Congress. *Library of Congress catalog record LCCN 2014034693: The iRest program for healing PTSD*. Library of Congress (MARC record, BooksAll 2016 dataset on Internet Archive). 2015. <https://archive.org/download/marc_loc_2016/BooksAll.2016.part41.utf8> (r5, confirmed, data) — Authoritative bibliographic data: Oakland, CA, New Harbinger, [2015]; viii + 205 pp., 23 cm; foreword by Eric Schoomaker MD PhD & Audrey Schoomaker RN (from the cover); includes bibliographical references; ISBNs for paperback, PDF and ePub. The pre-publication record (Nov 2014) explains Open Library's 2014 date. The URL is the dataset file; this record is at byte offset 192465532, length 3962.
- **[@ctgov]** U.S. National Library of Medicine. *ClinicalTrials.gov search: 'yoga nidra' (22 studies) and iRest (27 studies)*. 2026. <https://clinicaltrials.gov/search?term=%22yoga%20nidra%22> (r4, read, data/official, cited ×4) — Queried the v2 API on 2026-09-28: 22 'yoga nidra' records (15 completed, 2 withdrawn, 3 unknown); 11 with US sites, others Iceland, Turkey, UK, Italy, Taiwan. Few Indian trials: those are in CTRI

## Audio recordings

- **[@call]** Annie Payson Call; readers incl. Christine Rottger, Stacey Malcolm, Anita Sloma-Martinez. *Power Through Repose (LibriVox project 22851)*. LibriVox. <https://librivox.org/api/feed/audiobooks/?id=22851&format=json&extended=1> (r3, *unverified*) — 20 sections from the 1900 enlarged edition; as of Oct 2026 the API shows no audio files and unassigned sections, so it is apparently in progress.
- **[@birkholm2018]** Birkholm, M. *iRest: Integrative Restoration Yoga Nidra for Deep Relaxation*. The Great Courses. 2018. <https://www.overdrive.com/media/6032055/irest> (r3, read, practitioner/popular) — 24-class audio course by an iRest teacher, covering the 10 iRest steps plus sessions on sleep, pain, stress and trauma, with short and long practices. Not listened to; checked only to tell it apart from Miller's 2015 program. A second mainstream audio course on iRest.
- **[@brody2018]** Brody, K. *The Daring to Rest Yoga Nidra Meditation Program: A 40-Day Journey to Break the Cycle of Fatigue and Restore Vitality, Purpose, and Power*. Sounds True. 2018. <https://soundstrue.com/products/the-daring-to-rest-yoga-nidra-meditation-program/> (r3, confirmed, practitioner) — Separate ~5 h audio course (4:59:13; digital ISBN 978-1-68364-072-1, 6 CDs ISBN 978-1-68364-069-1), released March 2018. Four teaching sessions plus 8 yoga nidra meditations, one per five-day block of the book (track list seen in OverDrive sample player). Not bundled with the book. Sounds True store metadata tags it end-of-life Oct 2025.
- **[@chene2004]** Chéné, Patrick-André. *Sophrologie. Techniques de base : les cinq séances indispensables (CD)*. Ellébore. 2004. <https://catalogue.bnf.fr/ark:/12148/cb391107258> (r5, confirmed, fr) — BnF record: 1 CD, about 65 min, voiced by Chéné, collection Praxis
- **[@desai2019]** Desai, K. *Yoga Nidra: The Art of Transformational Sleep (unabridged audiobook, read by Siiri Scott)*. Tantor Audio. 2019. <https://www.blackstonelibrary.com/yoga-nidra> (r3, confirmed, practitioner) — Released 28 Feb 2019, about 15.5 h; library CD ISBN 9798200370139, MP3 CD 9798200370153. The bio gives degrees in anthropology and psychology.
- **[@desaie]** Desai, K. *21 Minute Rejuvenation (guided yoga nidra)*. Yoga Nidra Network. <https://www.yoganidranetwork.org/nidras/21-minute-rejuvenation/> (r3, confirmed, practitioner) — Free recording; the description says the IAM method is energy-based and uses counted breath and 61-point body rotation. The best evidence found for the Amrit stage techniques outside the book.
- **[@dinsmoretuli2022c]** Dinsmore-Tuli, U. & Tuli, N. *Yoga Nidra Made Easy Audio Files (companion recordings, 22 practices in female and male voices)*. Yoga Nidrā Network. 2022. <https://www.yoganidranetwork.org/yoga-nidra-made-easy-audio-files-page/> (r3, confirmed, practitioner) — Free, legitimate MP3s keyed to chapters. Tracks 1.0-10.8 match the Introduction and Ch 2-10; tracks 11.0-13.0 accompany the Ch 14 closing practices. Female tracks run about 9-28 min (about 6.5 h in total), measured from file headers, not listened to. Tracks 10.1-10.8 form a staged plan to wean off recordings.
- **[@dinsmoretuli2024]** Dinsmore-Tuli, U. (ed.). *Nidra Shakti Resources / Treasury of 108 Transcripts (companion recordings and transcripts)*. Yoga Nidra Network / Sitaram & Sons Ltd. 2024. <https://www.yoganidranetwork.org/nidra_shakti_resources/> (r4, confirmed, official) — Companion to the book. The full transcripts and audio unlock with the book's ISBN. Many Treasury recordings are also free in the public library at https://www.yoganidranetwork.org/nidras/, each with a publisher note giving length, date, setting, rotation style and contributor; some are marked unavailable or sit behind other paid products. Covers Satyananda-, Himalayan- and iRest-style rotations, Kavacam nyasa, hypnotic, children's, menstrual-cycle, menopause and therapeutic practices. Practices 66-77 use 'the same set of nine components'.

CD or subscription-app download. The album notes give the sankalpa as seed and watering, 'anything can fail you but not your sankalpa', effortless rotation, 'diseases can be cured'. Store page only; recording not heard.

- **[@kundig2023a]** Kündig, B. *Geführte Yoga Nidra Übung (hochdeutsch) – Folge 6, Umarmungen des Universums*. Satyananda-format without sound awareness or chidakasha. 'I stay awake' vow. Model sankalpa 'Ich lebe ruhig und gelassen', visualised written on paper. Rotation phrased as 'relax'. Breath counting 30->1 at belly, chest and nostrils (~10 min). Opposites heavy/light and cool/warm. 9 rapid images. Not verified as identical to the book's CD.
- **[@kundig2024]** Kündig, B. *Yoga Nidra in English – Folge 189, Umarmungen des Universums*. Closely follows Bihar-school script wording ('bound to come true', full rotation list). Counting 27->1 at navel, chest and nostrils. Chakra symbols. Opposites heavy/light and pain/pleasure. Speaker not stated.

Explains why she prefers 'safe and simple' to 'trauma-informed': heavy on choices and invitations, light on visualisation.

## Podcasts

- **[@arnett2021]** Arnett, M. *Radiant Rest: An Interview with Tracee Stanley*. The Mindful Minute. 2021. <https://www.merylarnett.com/podcast/radiant-rest-tracee-stanley> (r2, read, popular) — Show notes only; host is Stanley's student
- **[@bacharach2025]** Bacharach, T. *Darm mit Aussicht #77: Yoga Nidra: Dein Weg in die tiefe Entspannung – mit Dr. Lan Göttinger*. Darm mit Aussicht (gut-health podcast). 2025. <https://thomasbacharach.podigee.io/77-yoga-nidra-ein-weg-in-die-tiefe-entspannung> (r3, confirmed, practitioner/popular, de) — 4 Mar 2025, 41:18. Host is a GP. Covers sankalpa (optional, word or short sentence), sleep is fine and the hypnagogic state 'comparable' to hypnosis, brainwaves, length 10-60 min, history (once for advanced yogis; 'school of founders in the Himalaya'), iRest/NSDR (she keeps the name Yoga Nidra), and gut/parasympathetic claims. Transcribed from the audio.
- **[@baird2024]** Baird, C. *Journey to Balance: Insights from Tamara Skyhawk (Today's Balanced Leader with Caroline Baird)*. Today's Balanced Leader / Today's Evolving Leader (Captivate). 2024. <https://feeds.captivate.fm/todays-evolving-leader/> (r2, confirmed, practitioner/popular) — 31 May 2024, 39 min. Episode audio: https://podcasts.captivate.fm/media/3d35c996-3e58-4c92-9edd-e18ec4841319/journey-to-balance-insights-from-tamara-skyhawk.mp3 (transcribed locally). Author says she wrote vol. 1 in the 2020 lockdown as a resource for her trainees, goes off-script herself, and explains yoga nidra through a three-body/impressions (samskara) model: positive impressions dropped into the subconscious in deep relaxation.
- **[@balkaran2026]** Balkaran, R. (host). *Theo Wildcroft and Beverley Nolan, "Leading Safe and Simple Yoga Nidras: A Yoga Teacher's Guide" (Singing Dragon, 2026)*. New Books Network. 2026. <https://newbooksnetwork.com/leading-safe-and-simple-yoga-nidras> (r3, confirmed, practitioner/scholarly) — 27 Aug 2026, 41 min; the authors' first podcast on the book. Covers the book's genesis, a definition of yoga nidra (hypnagogia), the safety rationale (suggestible states), history (relaxationism to Satyananda), the critique of proprietary schools, QR audio, memorising rotation routes, three transcribed practices. Machine-transcribed for this summary.
- **[@belanger2020]** Belanger, E. *Sanctuary Meditation and Yoga Nidra, with Rod Stryker (On and Off Your Mat Yoga Podcast, Ep. 64)*. Acast. 2020. <https://shows.acast.com/5a5fdec3a4d96aa520f89227/episodes/sanctuary-meditation-of-yoga-nidra-with-rod-stryker> (r2, read, practitioner) — The show notes (host's takeaways, not a transcript) summarise Stryker: the body asleep, the mind in a delta pattern, a thread of consciousness; both practice and state. Audio not heard.
- **[@daringtorestpod]** Brody, Karen. *The Daring to Rest Podcast*. <https://open.spotify.com/show/5HKIFbOq8ikKbd9QSX6fJ0> (r2, confirmed, popular, cited ×2)
- **[@naropamiller]** Devine, David (host); Miller, Richard. *Mindful U Episode 49: Richard Miller, Integrative Restoration, Yoga Nidra*. Naropa University. <https://www.naropa.edu/podcast/richard-miller-integrative-restoration-yoga-nidra/> (r3, read, popular, cited ×6) — Miller's own account of his path and the 2004 Walter Reed renaming of yoga nidra as iRest
- **[@umagratefullife]** Dinsmore-Tuli, Uma. *Uma Dinsmore-Tuli – On Yoga Nidrā Shakti (A Grateful Life)*. 2025. <https://podcasts.apple.com/us/podcast/uma-dinsmore-tuli-on-yoga-nidr%C4%81-shakti/id1193048930?i=1000700534069> (r3, confirmed, popular, cited ×1) — Interview on Nidra Shakti and post-lineage practice
- **[@gottinger2025a]** Göttinger, L. *gerngesund #117: Die Kunst der tiefen Entspannung: Yoga Nidra für Deinen Alltag*. gerngesund podcast (Human of Health, Podigee). 2025. <https://gerngesund.podigee.io/117-117> (r3, confirmed, practitioner/popular, de) — Author's own launch episode (20 Mar 2025, 29:36). She reads the book's 'Hormonelle Balance bei Frauen' passage (citing Rani 2013) and the opening of 'Die ersten Schritte', and guides the book's ~12-min 'Yoga Nidra für schnelle Entspannung' in full. Transcribed by me from the audio.
- **[@gottinger2023]** Göttinger, L. *gerngesund #99: Wundersamer Vagusnerv – Hype oder Gesundheitssegen?* gerngesund podcast (Human of Health, Podigee). 2023. <https://gerngesund.podigee.io/99-099> (r3, confirmed, popular/practitioner, de) — 12 Oct 2023, 27:50. Pre-book. She presents polyvagal theory as an explanatory model that 'has not gone uncriticised', and is sceptical of vagus-nerve device hype. Useful to anticipate the book's nervous-system chapter (not read).
- **[@lusk2022a]** Julie Lusk; host Trey Downes. *Yoga Nidra (Guided Meditation) - Julie Lusk, M.Ed., RYT E500 (Your Superior Self podcast)*. Your Superior Self (Libsyn). 2022. <https://yoursuperiorself.libsyn.com/yoga-nidra-guided-meditation-julie-lusk-med-ryt-e500> (r3, confirmed, practitioner) — 10 Jan 2022, 48.6 min, full guided practice transcribed locally. Lusk introduces it as the 'heartfelt' script from her 2021 book Yoga Nidra Meditations (not the 2015 book). Used for cue forms, the example body-tour stage, props, and her stance on drifting.
- **[@kyle2021]** Kyle, J. *Tracee Stanley on Yoga Nidra (#127)*. Embodied Philosophy (Chitheads podcast). 2021. <https://www.embodiedphilosophy.com/podcasts/tracee-stanley-on-yoga-nidra-127> (r3, read, practitioner) — 5 Feb 2021; show notes only read: goddess Yoga Nidra, four states, state vs technique, trauma and safety, nest set-up
- **[@kyle2017]** Kyle, Jacob (host); Miller, Richard C. *Richard C. Miller on Yoga Nidra (#60)*. Embodied Philosophy. 2017. <https://www.embodiedphilosophy.com/?p=2645> (r3, read, practitioner/popular) — 28 Oct 2017. Topics: iRest components, PTSD, the koshas and identification. The bio credits Jean Klein, T.K.V. Desikachar and Stephen Chang. Only the show notes were read.
- **[@kundig2023]** Kündig, B. *Was ist Yoga Nidra (und wie es dir nützt) – Folge 4, Umarmungen des Universums*. Umarmungen des Universums (Spotify for Podcasters / Anchor). 2023. <https://podcasters.spotify.com/pod/show/barbarakuendig/episodes/Was-ist-Yoga-Nidra-und-wie-es-dir-ntzt---Folge-4-e228bdj> (r3, read, practitioner, de) — 8-min author talk, transcribed. Learned yoga nidra in India 20+ years ago and practises daily. It does not mean you should stop sleeping; practise once or twice a day. Alpha/theta: feeling asleep is only twilight sleep, and you still absorb the instructions. Mentions Himalayan yogis who barely sleep.
- **[@kundig2023b]** Kündig, Barbara. *Geführte Yoga Nidra Übung (hochdeutsch) - Folge 6 (Barbara Kündig podcast), audio file from RSS enclosure*. 2023. <https://anchor.fm/s/dd85612c/podcast/play/68480556/https%3A%2F%2Fd3ctxlq1ktw2nl.cloudfront.net%2Fstaging%2F2023-3-12%2F5b9c5ebb-2fa7-8098-dcbf-5cd93a56b10d.mp3> (r3, confirmed, de) — Re-transcribed 0:00-1:15, 3:20-4:30, 10:00-12:00 with faster-whisper for the language excerpt (sankalpa at 3:37, 30->1 counting cue at 11:30, stillness and eyes-closed instructions at 0:54-1:08). Same episode as the earlier Spotify link.

Transcribed from the audio in the feed https://anchor.fm/s/4f364c88/podcast/rss.

Katja Schendel Sound Therapy Podcast. 2021. <https://www.katjaschendel.com/podcast/anna-roecker-musiktherapie-guided-imagery-and-music-yoga-nidra-meditation-alchemie-archetypen-achtsamkeit-musik> (r2, confirmed, practitioner, de) — About 51 min, recorded Feb 2021; yoga nidra at about 22-27 and 35-42 min. Heard via my own machine transcript (Whisper base), so details are approximate. Covers how the book began (an Ulm teacher trainee's husband recovering from alcoholism; publisher's offer c. 2006), the savasana-vs-yoga-nidra classroom comparison, 'the unconscious must agree' with the sankalpa, GIM music used to find a sankalpa, and her 'Lösung' gloss of sankalpa.

Only show notes read, no transcript.
- **[@matter2022]** Sensitive Matter. *Kamini Desai: The Art of Yoga Nidra (Sensitive Matter podcast)*. Podbean. 2022. <https://sensitivematters.podbean.com/e/kamini-desai-the-art-of-yoga-nidra> (r2, read, practitioner) — 20 Dec 2022; show notes only (yoga nidra as 'meditation made easy', her background). No transcript.
- **[@simon2015a]** Simon, T.; Miller, R. C. *Welcoming Whatever Arises (Insights at the Edge, interview with Richard Miller about iRest Meditation)*. Sounds True. 2015. <https://resources2.soundstrue.com/podcast/welcoming-whatever-arises/> (r3, confirmed, practitioner) — 68 min, posted 10 Nov 2015 to promote the iRest Meditation audio program. Full official transcript at https://resources2.soundstrue.com/transcript/welcoming-whatever-arises/. Covers how iRest arose (1970 class; Walter Reed, which he dates here to 2004); inner resource paired with negative emotions; opposites of emotion and belief; the 'everything is a messenger' and 'law of awareness' principles; unreferenced MRI and brain-network claims. Includes a short guided practice: hands, then opposites, then widened attention.
- **[@simon2011]** Simon, Tami (host); Miller, Richard C. *Yoga Nidra: The Sleep Yoga (Insights at the Edge)*. Sounds True. 2011. <https://www.soundstrue.com/a/resources/transcript/yoga-nidra-the-sleep-yoga/> (r3, confirmed, practitioner/popular) — Full free transcript, 11 Jan 2011, about the book. Miller sets out the stages (heartfelt desire = sankalpa, intention, Inner Resource, mouth-first body scan, breath, emotions, beliefs, joy, awareness, integration), the want/need/action questions, the shravana/manana/nididhyasana framing, and his sleep claims (snoring yet aware; 'paradoxical sleep'; awareness through deep sleep). Key primary source for debate 3, but its neuroscience claims are weak.
- **[@simon2015]** Simon, Tami (host); Miller, Richard C. *Welcoming Whatever Arises (Insights at the Edge)*. Sounds True. 2015. <https://www.soundstrue.com/a/resources/podcast/welcoming-whatever-arises/> (r3, read, practitioner/popular) — 10 Nov 2015, 68 min: iRest for military trauma, core ideas, a guided practice on opposites. Only the show notes were read; a transcript link exists.
