## Courses

- **[@boothroydytt]** Boothroyd, Ally. *Transformational Sleep Online Yoga Nidra 100-Hour YTT*. <https://allyboothroyd.com/> (r2, read, practitioner, cited ×2) — 10-week online 100-h training + Level 2; Satyananda-style 'systematic method'
- **[@daringtorestacademy]** Brody, Karen. *Daring to Rest Leadership Academy (facilitator training)*. <http://daringtorest.com/> (r2, read, practitioner, cited ×3) — Online facilitator/rest-coach training for women; free monthly sessions; facilitator directory. Hours not stated
- **[@desaic]** Desai, K. *I AM Yoga Nidra Immersion (Amrit Yoga Institute course page)*. Amrit Yoga Institute. <https://amrityoga.org/i-am-yoga-nidra-immersion/> (r2, confirmed, industry) — 50 h, 15 modules (Realization; Stilling Leads to Seeing; Kleshas; Integration & Energetic Diffusion Technique; Koshas; Karma, Samskaras & Dis-identification; Secondary/Primary Intention; Structure of Yoga Nidra). Claims a 'five-tool approach' and that neuroscience has proven 45 min = 3 h of sleep. States that Amrit Desai appointed Kamini a successor in the Lakulish lineage.
- **[@desaid]** Desai, K. *Yoga Nidra I: Immersion (I AM Education self-paced syllabus)*. I AM Education (TalentLMS). <https://iameducation.talentlms.com/catalog/info/id:125> (r3, confirmed, practitioner) — Full module list including 'Secondary Intention' and 'Primary Intention' modules. States that Desai's book is strongly recommended for the Immersion and required for the Certification track.
- **[@iamcert]** Desai, Kamini & Vosler, John. *I AM Yoga Nidra Certification*. Amrit Yoga Institute. <https://amrityoga.org/on-demand-courses/i-am-yoga-nidra-certification/> (r3, read, practitioner, cited ×1) — Four tiers (Practice, Immersion, Certification, Advanced); certification 50 h, Zoom + home study; card-deck system for tailoring; on-demand $799
- **[@totalyntt]** Dinsmore-Tuli, Uma & Tuli, Nirlipta. *Total Yoga Nidra Teacher & Facilitator Training*. Yoga Nidrā Network. <https://www.yoganidranetwork.org/teacher-training/> (r3, read, practitioner, cited ×3) — 36 h (12 x 3 h online, or hybrid/residential); IAYT Approved Professional Development; teaches several schools side by side
- **[@himalayanyncourse]** Himalayan Institute (Sovik, Rolf et al.). *The Complete Practice of Yoga Nidra (online course)*. <https://himalayaninstitute.org/online/the-complete-practice-of-yoga-nidra/> (r3, *unverified*, practitioner, cited ×1) — Himalayan-tradition course; page returned 403, contents not checked
- **[@irestlevel1]** iRest Institute. *iRest Level 1 Training*. <https://shop.irest.org/pages/about-level-1> (r3, read, practitioner, cited ×3) — Entry training in the 10-step iRest protocol; ~30 CE hours; no prerequisites; leads to Level 2 and certification; required reading Miller 2010
- **[@lusk2023a]** Lusk, J. T. *Yoga Nidra for Complete Relaxation and Stress Relief (online workshop, Jan 2023)*. Satchidananda Ashram-Yogaville (Yogaville Online). 2023. <https://online.yogaville.org/courses/yoga-nidra-for-complete-relaxation-and-stress-relief-2023> (r2, confirmed, industry) — Workshop with the book's title. Promises 'six amazing levels of relaxation' via pratyahara, permanent personality change via sankalpa, and '1 hour = 4 hours of sleep'. Handouts include an Assess Your Stress worksheet and a brainwave chart. Part of a 40-hour certification.
- **[@irestimmersion]** Miller, Richard & Lopez, Stephanie. *iRest Yoga Nidra Immersion (online course)*. Sounds True. <https://product.soundstrue.com/yoga-nidra/> (r3, read, practitioner/industry, cited ×1) — 8-week video course for practitioners; workbook, Q&A, 14 CE credits
- **[@moore2020d]** Moore, S. *Waking Up with the Yoga of Sleep (online yoga nidra course)*. Scott Moore Yoga on Teachable. 2020s. <https://scott-moore-yoga.teachable.com/p/yoga-nidra-course> (r2, confirmed, industry) — Paid course (~12 h video, 20+ practices). Module titles show his framework: koshas, Shiva/Shakti myth, non-dualism, stages and states of consciousness, science. Part of a 70-hour Yoga Alliance CE teacher training.
- **[@divinesleeptt]** Reis, Jennifer. *Divine Sleep Yoga Nidra Teacher Training*. Kripalu Center. <https://kripalu.org/presenters-programs/divine-sleep-yoga-nidra-teacher-training> (r3, *unverified*, practitioner, cited ×1) — 40-h, 5-day residential certificate at Kripalu; 200+ page script manual; creator claims 2,500+ graduates (search snippet; page 403)
- **[@rishiyogashalabonnas2026]** Rishi Yoga Shala (Bonnasse, P.; Bharucha, K.). *Formation Professeur de Yoga-Nidra & Méditation, 120 heures (Rishikesh / Pyrénées)*. Rishi Yoga Shala. 2026. <https://www.rishiyogashala.com/formation-yoga-inde/formation-nidra-inde/> (r3, confirmed, practitioner/official, fr) — Detailed 4-module programme (jñāna, haṭha, rāja, tantra) of the training that uses Bonnasse 2019 as its main study text (see /formation-yoga-inde/formation-yoga-nidra-inde-francais/ and /mediatheque/yoga-nidra-108-pratiques/). The school says it is validated by the FFEY. Teaching fee €1400; next session 25 Oct–11 Nov 2026. The best available proxy for the book's contents.
- **[@institute2026]** Santosha Yoga Institute. *Santosha Yoga Institute: Yoga Nidra 50-Hour Online Teacher Training (lead instructor Barbara Kundig) / Bali Yoga Nidra Teacher Training*. Santosha Yoga Institute (listed on Mind Is the Master). 2026. <https://mindisthemaster.com/courses/santosha-yoga-institute-yoga-nidra-50-hour-online-teacher-training/> (r2, read, industry) — Yoga Alliance YACEP 50-h online training, USD 439, 6 modules (origins, techniques, Tantra/Yoga Sutras/chakras, health and sleep, kids, teaching). Bali training page: https://santoshayogainstitute.edu.au/bali-yoga-nidra-teacher-training/.
- **[@skyhawk2025]** Skyhawk, T. *Yoga Nidra Teacher Training with Tamara Skyhawk (30-hr, Yoga Alliance CE)*. Skyhawk Yoga / Yoga Ghar. n.d. (2025 dates announced). <https://www.tamaraskyhawk.com/yoga-nidra-tt> (r2, confirmed, industry/practitioner) — Uses Yoga Nidra Scripts as the practice text; 70-page manual with script-writing tips and charts of states/koshas/stages; covers Ayurveda (doshas, seasons) tailoring. Claims the practice reshapes personality, tendencies, karma and destiny. Lists her influences (Oxford 'School' for Hindu Studies, Vasant Lad, Swami Sarvapriyananda).
- **[@stanley2022a]** Stanley, T. *Radiant Rest Retreat: Yoga Nidra, Self-Inquiry, Sankalpa (Esalen workshop, May 2022)*. Esalen Institute. 2022. <https://www.esalen.org/past-workshops/radiant-rest-retreat-yoga-nidra-self-inquiry-sankalpa> (r3, confirmed, practitioner) — Shows her 'Perfect Sankalpa' (resolve formed without doubt) and 40-day take-home sankalpa practice, a framing stronger than in the book itself
- **[@empoweredwisdomtt]** Stanley, Tracee & Tacoronte-Perez, Chanti. *Empowered Wisdom Yoga Nidra Teacher Training*. <https://www.traceestanley.com/yoga-nidra-trainings> (r3, read, practitioner, cited ×1) — 100 h online incl. 10-h 'Subtle Body Wisdom' course; yoga nidra taught as a state; next cohort 2027
- **[@parayoganidra]** Stryker, Rod. *ParaYoga Nidra: A 10-Hour Immersion in Enlightened Sleep*. Yoga International. <https://yogainternational.com/ecourse/parayoga-nidra/> (r3, *unverified*, practitioner, cited ×2) — Himalayan/Sri Vidya-derived ParaYoga approach; Stryker also runs the 'Sanctuary' app. Page did not render
- **[@sturzebecher2026b]** Stürzebecher, N. & Krastev, G. *Yoga Nidra – Ascent into the Subconscious (four 90-min online classes, May 2026)*. Purple Cloud Institute (Thinkific). 2026. <https://purplecloudinstitute.thinkific.com/courses/Yoga_Nidra%E2%80%93Ascent_into_the_Subconscious> (r3, confirmed, industry) — US$150. Classes: philosophical and yogic foundations; Satyananda's method and focus; the stages; effects and contemporary applications. Facilitator bios for the author and Georgi Krastev (philologist, Esoteric Buddhism/tantra).
- **[@tcsptrance]** The Chicago School of Professional Psychology. *Yoga Nidra & Hypnosis: Yogic Trance or Trance Logic? (continuing education)*. <https://tcsppofficeofce.com/courses/yoga-nidra-hypnosis-yogic-trance-or-trance-logic/> (r2, confirmed, practitioner/scholarly, cited ×1) — CE course for psychologists; via web search, not opened
- **[@urlacher2026d]** Urlacher, S. *40-HR Live Online Yoga Nidra Teacher Training (and 35-HR Self-Paced YNTT)*. Sacred Nature Yoga (Yoga Alliance YACEP). 2026. <https://www.yinandmeditation.com/yoga-nidra-training-with-sagel/yoga-nidra-teacher-training-live-online> (r3, confirmed, industry) — Required reading: Urlacher's two books plus Satyananda's Yoga Nidra. Covers koshas, neuroscience, sequencing, trauma-informed guidance and script development. 12 participants, $349-419, 40 Yoga Alliance CE hours. Self-paced version: https://www.yinandmeditation.com/yoga-nidra-training-with-sagel/self-paced-yoga-nidra-teacher-training
- **[@vedabharati2014b]** Veda Bharati, Swami. *Silence, Shavasana Practices and Yoga Nidra Retreat 2015 (syllabus), AHYMSIN Newsletter May 2014*. AHYMSIN / Swami Rama Sadhaka Grama. 2014. <https://www.ahymsin.org/docs2/News/1405May/02.html> (r3, confirmed, practitioner/official) — The full Himalayan preparatory sequence: makarasana, diaphragmatic breathing, shavasana, Exercise without Movement, tension-release, subtler relaxations, 31/61 points (blue star, mantra), 4 whole-body breath patterns, quick conscious rest, OM kriya, yoga nidra proper, delta explanation, how to come out. Also lists recommended readings and audios.
- **[@wildcroft2026a]** Wildcroft, T. *LIVE COURSE: Leading Safe and Simple Yoga Nidras*. Theo's Substack. 2026. <https://theowildcroft.substack.com/p/live-course-leading-safe-and-simple-bad> (r3, read, practitioner/industry) — Online, 5 x 3 h, GBP 195, small group; ends with an assessed recording of your own nidra; YACEP CPD and an American Yoga Council yoga nidra badge. Runs Nov-Dec 2026; earlier runs in 2023 (Yoga Reading, 16 h) and Jan-Feb 2026.
- **[@wildcroft2023]** Wildcroft, T. *Yoga Nidra Training with Theo Wildcroft (16hrs YA certified)*. Yoga Reading. 2023. <https://yogareading.punchpass.com/series/22382> (r2, read, practitioner) — Curriculum outline that prefigures the book: how rest is experienced and why rest is political; history from pre-modern origins; esoteric maps; a simple, safe structure.
- **[@wildcroft2026b]** Wildcroft, T. & Nolan, B. *FREE Book Launch: 'Leading Safe and Simple Yoga Nidras: A Yoga Teacher's Guide'*. Yogacampus. 2026. <https://www.yogacampus.com/courses/cpd-specialist-training-events/nidra-book-launch> (r2, read, practitioner/community) — Free online event, 11 Dec 2026, 1.25 h, recorded; book introduction plus a two-voice yoga nidra.
- **[@horizon2026]** Yoga Horizon. *Formation Yoga Nidra (12-module, two-year curriculum)*. Yoga Horizon, Lyon. 2026. <https://www.yoga-horizon.fr/formation-approfondie-en-yoga-nidra-a-lyon/> (r3, confirmed, practitioner, fr) — A Natha/Shaiva-lineage 'traditional' yoga nidra quite unlike the Satyananda script. Mostly sleep-entry and dream practices: falling asleep on the internal sound and the active nostril, lying slow kapalabhati with suspensions, dreaming in the right then left eye, an 'animal' practice, nyasa on the heart, using night awakenings. Says relaxation is not the goal. Useful for the 'what does traditional mean' question.

## Apps

- **[@desai2018]** Desai, K. *IAM Yoga Nidra (App Store listing)*. Apple App Store. 2018. <https://apps.apple.com/us/app/id1265932217> (r2, confirmed, industry) — One-time-purchase app: Healing Shift 36 min, Release Reaction 29, Rapid Recharge 22, Self Expression 29 (visual intention), plus later additions; 'intention options'. Description claims 45 min = ~3 h sleep, serotonin/cortisol/blood-sugar effects, and '8 weeks' and '11 hours' brain changes. Calls the method energy-based. Separate from the I AM Being app (iambeingapp).
- **[@nsdrapp]** Guerra Santana, B. *NSDR Yoga-Nidra (iOS app)*. 2024. <https://apps.apple.com/us/app/nsdr-yoga-nidra/id6504159193> (r1, read, industry, cited ×3) — Read App Store page: 'scientifically-backed', binaural beats, $14.99/month; no ratings. Example of the NSDR app genre
- **[@hagman2012]** Hagman, M. G. *Yoga Nidra (iOS app)*. Mindfulness Apps Sweden AB. 2012. <https://apps.apple.com/us/app/-/id573151889> (r2, read, practitioner/industry) — Guided practices recorded by a Satyananda Yoga Sweden / IYFM teacher; describes the method as developed by Satyananda. Year is approximate (app id range); verify.
- **[@stryker]** Stryker, R. *Sanctuary with Rod Stryker*. ParaYoga, Inc. <https://apps.apple.com/us/app/sanctuary-with-rod-stryker/id1426508272> (r2, confirmed, industry) — Subscription app ($9.99/month or $89.99/year) with yoga nidra ('Enlightened Sleep'), meditation and deep-sleep tracks; 4.9 from 221 ratings. Compendium 10 cites it under parayoganidra; it may deserve its own entry.

## Tools

- **[@satyamyogaprasad]** Bihar School of Yoga / Yoga Publications Trust. *Satyam Yoga Prasad (Bihar School of Yoga digital library app)*. <https://www.biharyoga.net/satyam-yoga-prasad.php> (r4, read, practitioner, cited ×4) — Free app/web library (satyamyogaprasad.net) of the Bihar tradition's publications, audio and video; hosts the yoga nidra recordings in ~100 languages
- **[@iambeingapp]** Desai, Kamini. *I AM Being – Yoga Nidra 4 Rest (app)*. <https://apps.apple.com/us/app/i-am-being-yoga-nidra-4-rest/id1642778091> (r2, confirmed, industry/practitioner, cited ×2) — Subscription app of the I AM / Amrit method; 20-45 min nidras, kids nidras. Seen only in search results
- **[@insighttimer]** Insight Timer. *Insight Timer: Yoga Nidra topic page*. <https://insighttimer.com/meditation-topics/yoga_nidra> (r2, read, industry/popular, cited ×7) — Largest free meditation library; editorially curated yoga nidra list (Piercy, Boothroyd, Kate James...). Tracks not clinically studied (as the page itself notes)

Says she trained as a yoga teacher with Sri Mahesh, 'pioneer of Hatha Yoga in France'. Age 56 in 2006. Its 'contact with yoga 18 years ago' conflicts with her teaching since 1981.

- **[@sturzebecher2025b]** Stürzebecher, N. *yoga-nidrā im inklusiven Kontext: Tiefenentspannung, Regulation & Selbsterforschung (workshop handout, 23 Nov 2025)*. yoganidra.at (author's site). 2025. <https://yoganidra.at/wp-content/uploads/Yoga-Nidra-im-Inklusiven-Kontext-2025.pdf> (r3, confirmed, practitioner, de) — 21 pp. The 8 Satyananda stages with graded introduction (essential 1-3-8; basic 1-2-3-4-7-8; complete); 20-min minimum, 40-45 min full; detailed posture and inclusion adaptations (eyes open for PTSD etc., matsya-kridasana, wheelchair, amputation, visual impairment); prohibitions (no music, bowls, eye pillows, touch); contraindications. Pp. 16-21 summarise research 'documented in my book' with strong claims (HIV CD4, asthma, delta with full awareness). Useful for adaptations; treat the research claims critically.
- **[@sturzebecher2026a]** Stürzebecher, N. *Yoga Nidrā Ausbildung mit Zertifikat in der originalen Yoga-Nidrā-Technik nach Svāmī Satyānanda (training brochure)*. yoganidra.at. 2026. <https://yoganidra.at/wp-content/uploads/yoga-nidra.pdf> (r3, confirmed, industry, de) — 12 pp. One-year, 5-weekend BSY-authorised training in Vienna (2026-27, EUR 1,950). Topics mirror the book's Part I; author's training handbook is c. 330 pp. Sankalpa work via daily review, spiritual diary, SWAN method. Literature: Satyananda's Yoga Nidra (German ed.) and Prakashananda's Yoga Nidra – Neue Erkenntnisse.
- **[@trokes2014]** Trökes, A. *Yoga Nidra: Die Yoga-Tiefenentspannung - Leseprobe (publisher reading sample, 8 pp.: covers, full TOC, pp. 6-7, 26-27, 48-49, 54-55, 74-75)*. Gräfe und Unzer (GU) / HarperCollins Deutschland. 2014. <https://pondus-harpercollins.s3.eu-central-1.amazonaws.com/public/NCOM3d523bs29hUZbEoNcxwrTM5ISfSP1GdHIsWrQYB/9783833829338.pdf> (r4, confirmed, practitioner, de) — Official publisher sample linked as 'Zur Leseprobe' from gu.de/products/57521-yoga-nidra. Shows the first 4 of the 'ten phases', the sankalpa examples, the vagus box mapping each stage to brain regions, posture pages and two guided meditations.
- **[@london2025]** University College London. *Yoga Nidra for Insomnia and Posttraumatic Stress Symptoms (NCT06888336)*. ClinicalTrials.gov. 2025. <https://clinicaltrials.gov/study/NCT06888336> (r4, read, data) — Randomized feasibility study of two virtual iRest yoga nidra variants vs. waitlist, n = 60, completed 2025, no results posted (as of Oct 2026). The only registered randomized iRest trial on post-traumatic symptoms found. Seen via the v2 API; the sponsor is named from a search snippet.
- **[@urlacher2025]** Urlacher, S. (ABNewswire). *Beloved Yoga Nidra Script Book Continues to Inspire Teachers, Practitioners, and Wellness Providers Worldwide*. ABNewswire (syndicated, e.g. Sherbrooke Record business feed). 2025. <https://business.sherbrookerecord.com/sherbrookerecord/article/abnewswire-2025-6-5-beloved-yoga-nidra-script-book-continues-to-inspire-teachers-practitioners-and-wellness-providers-worldwide> (r1, confirmed, industry) — Paid self-promotional release (5 June 2025). Source of the claims 'consistently top-ranked on Amazon's Yoga list' and 'widely adopted in teacher trainings', neither independently verified. Shows how self-published yoga nidra books are marketed.
- **[@vedabharati2004]** Veda Bharati, Swami. *Yoga Nidra Refined Silence (lecture, Rishikesh 2004)*. AHYMSIN (posted 2009). 2004. <https://www.ahymsin.org/yoga-nidra-refined-silence/> (r3, confirmed, practitioner/official) — Full free lecture. Says recordings are preparation, not yoga nidra; mastery means entering the state without the technique; the dark heart cave; Vedanta and Yoga Sutra views of sleep. Ends with a ~5-min guided practice (body check from the forehead, crown-to-centre breaths, heart-centre breath, dark cave). Includes unsupported health claims.
